Our approach dynamically manages agent collaboration through soft state transitions, implemented via two key components: (1) State-Aware Policy Learning: Agent states are divided into ``Active'', ``Standby'', and ``Terminated'' states, selectively propagating messages based on agent memory. The policy employs a risk estimator to optimize agent state transitions by assessing hallucination risk, minimizing the influence of unreliable nodes while safeguarding valuable ones. (2) State-Aware Edge Optimization: Subgraph edges are pruned according to states learned from the policy, permanently removing ``Terminated'' nodes and retaining ``Standby'' nodes for subsequent rounds to assess their potential future contributions. Extensive experiments on general reasoning, domain-specific, and hallucination challenge tasks show that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines and significantly reduces token consumption through state-aware agent scheduling.

</details>


### [37] [AMATA: Adaptive Multi-Agent Trajectory Alignment for Knowledge-Intensive Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17352)
*Taolin Zhang,Dongyang Li,Chen Chen,Qizhou Chen,Jiuheng Wan,Xiaofeng He,Chengyu Wang,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AMATA是一个自适应多智能体轨迹对齐框架，通过六个专业智能体协作和轨迹偏好学习，在知识密集型问答中减少幻觉、提升事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识密集型问答中存在幻觉和长尾知识缺口问题，现有方法缺乏有效的多智能体协作与外部知识动态整合机制。

Method: 提出AMATA框架，包含六个专业智能体协作推理；将多智能体协作形式化为轨迹偏好对齐问题；引入智能体感知定制和智能体间偏好协调；提出轨迹内偏好学习（Intra-Trajectory Preference Learning）和智能体间依赖学习（Inter-Agent Dependency Learning），后者通过依赖感知直接偏好优化技术捕获跨智能体工具依赖。

Result: 在五个知识密集型QA基准上，AMATA一致优于基线方法、知识增强框架和基于LLM的轨迹系统；进一步分析表明该方法能有效降低token消耗。

Conclusion: AMATA通过多智能体协作与偏好对齐机制有效提升了知识密集型问答的事实一致性和可解释性，同时降低了计算开销，为多智能体系统的优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite substantial advances in large language models (LLMs), generating factually consistent responses for knowledge-intensive question answering remains challenging. These difficulties are primarily due to hallucinations and the limitations of LLMs in bridging long-tail knowledge gaps. To address this, we propose AMATA, an Adaptive Multi-Agent Trajectory Alignment framework that dynamically integrates external knowledge to improve response interpretability and factual grounding. Our architecture leverages six specialized agents that collaboratively perform structured actions for complex question reasoning. We formalize multi-agent collaboration with external tools as a trajectory preference alignment problem, incorporating question-aware agent customization and inter-agent preference harmonization. AMATA introduces two principal innovations: (1) Intra-Trajectory Preference Learning, which learns objective-oriented preferences to prioritize critical agents, and (2) Inter-Agent Dependency Learning, which captures cross-agent tool dependencies through a novel dependency-aware direct preference optimization technique. Empirical results show that AMATA consistently outperforms baseline approaches, knowledge-augmented frameworks, and LLM-based trajectory systems on five established knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks. Further analysis demonstrates the efficiency of our method in reducing token consumption.

</details>


### [38] [Learning Transferable Topology Priors for Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17359)
*Taolin Zhang,Zijie Zhou,Jiuheng Wan,Tingyuan Hu,Chengyu Wang,Xiaofeng He,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TopoPrior提出从离线多领域参考协作图中学习可迁移的拓扑先验，用于生成查询条件初始协作图，从而将拓扑搜索从在线优化转移至离线先验学习，减少在线推理开销和token消耗，提升多智能体LLM协作的跨领域可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体拓扑演化方法对每个查询从头构建或优化协作拓扑，导致大量在线搜索开销、高推理阶段token消耗以及在多领域场景下扩展性受限。

Method: TopoPrior包含两个核心模块：(1) 可迁移拓扑先验学习模块，采用条件变分图框架在潜在空间中捕获跨领域的可复用结构规律；(2) 查询条件潜在适应模块，引入对抗对齐以减少不必要的领域差异，同时保留查询相关的结构变化。

Result: 在多领域推理基准上，TopoPrior一致性地提升了多种异构拓扑演化backbone的性能，同时减少了在线推理阶段的token使用量，且仅增加少量可训练参数。

Conclusion: 可迁移的拓扑初始化是一种有效且轻量的机制，能够提升跨领域多智能体LLM协作的效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems have shown strong potential for complex reasoning by coordinating specialized agents through structured communication. However, existing topology-evolution methods typically construct or optimize a collaboration topology for each query from scratch, leading to substantial online search overhead, high inference-time token consumption, and limited scalability in multi-domain settings. We propose TopoPrior, a framework for learning transferable topology priors for multi-agent LLM collaboration across domains. Rather than repeatedly searching for effective collaboration structures online, TopoPrior learns reusable topology priors from reference collaboration graphs collected offline from multiple domains and uses them to generate query-conditioned initial collaboration graphs for downstream refinement. By shifting part of topology search from per-query online optimization to offline prior learning, TopoPrior amortizes search cost while remaining compatible with existing topology-evolution backbones. Technically, TopoPrior contains two key components. First, a transferable topology prior learning module employs a conditional variational graph framework to capture reusable structural regularities across domains in a latent space. Second, a query-conditioned latent adaptation module introduces adversarial alignment to reduce unnecessary domain discrepancy while preserving query-relevant structural variation. Experiments on multi-domain reasoning benchmarks show that TopoPrior consistently improves several heterogeneous topology-evolution backbones while reducing online inference-time token usage, with only modest additional trainable parameters. These results suggest that transferable topology initialization is an effective and lightweight mechanism for improving the efficiency of multi-agent LLM collaboration across domains.

</details>


### [39] [NewsLens: A Multi-Agent Framework for Adversarial News Bias Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17364)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NewsLens 是一个五智能体对抗性流水线，通过结构化框架映射（事实验证、渐进/保守框架分析、宣传检测、中立总结）来检测新闻偏见，超越了传统分类方法。在15篇文章上的评估显示，中心媒体视角分歧度最高（PDS: 0.907），保守媒体操纵指数最高（MI: 0.600），跨模型一致性良好，但统计显著性受限于样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有媒体偏见检测局限于分类任务（如给文章打政治标签），只能识别偏见存在与否，无法揭示偏见的位置、表现形式以及结构性的信息缺失。因此需要一种更细粒度、可解释的偏见分析方法。

Method: 构建五智能体对抗性流水线，包括事实验证器、渐进框架分析器、保守框架分析器、宣传检测器和中立总结器，协同将文章解构为可解释的框架映射。使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Mistral 7B进行评估，采用视角分歧度（PDS）和操纵指数（MI）等量化指标。

Result: 中心媒体平均PDS最高（Qwen 0.907, Mistral 0.729），保守媒体平均MI最高（0.600）。高宣传内容跨模型一致性高（Republic World delta-PDS=0.125, MI均为0.8）。Mann-Whitney U检验因样本量小（n=15）未发现显著组间差异。消融实验表明移除宣传检测器会降低中立总结的遗漏精度。

Conclusion: NewsLens将基于词法-几何的偏见研究扩展为智能体LLM推理，实现了对新闻偏见的结构化、可解释分析，且完全可复现（开源模型、无需API密钥）。但需更大样本量验证统计显著性。

Abstract: Media bias detection has predominantly been framed as a classification task: assign a political label to an article or outlet. We argue this framing is too shallow: it identifies that bias exists but not where, how, or crucially, what is structurally omitted. We present NewsLens, a five-agent adversarial pipeline for structured news bias navigation. A Fact Verifier, Progressive Framing Analyst, Conservative Framing Analyst, Propaganda Detector, and Neutral Summarizer collaborate to deconstruct articles into interpretable framing maps, exposing ideological omissions, rhetorical manipulation, and framing boundaries. The system is evaluated on 15 articles across four geopolitical event clusters (India-Pakistan Kashmir, Gaza, Climate Policy, Ukraine) using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct (4-bit quantised, Google Colab T4), with cross-model validation using Mistral 7B on the Kashmir cluster. Center outlets show the highest mean Perspective Divergence Score (PDS: Qwen 0.907, Mistral 0.729 on Kashmir subset); conservative-framing outlets show the highest mean Manipulation Index (MI: 0.600 across both models). Cross-model comparison shows high consistency for high-propaganda content (Republic World delta-PDS=0.125, MI=0.8 both models) and greater variance for nuanced reporting. Mann-Whitney U tests find no statistically significant between-group differences at n=15, reported honestly as a sample-size limitation confirmed by post-hoc power analysis. A partial ablation removing the Propaganda Detector shows degraded omission precision in the Neutral Summarizer output. The architecture extends prior lexical-geometric bias work to agentic LLM reasoning, and is fully reproducible using open-weight models without API keys.

</details>


### [40] [Learning Faster with Better Tokens: Parameter-Efficient Vocabulary Adaptation for Specialized Text Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17379)
*Gunjan Balde,Soumyadeep Roy,Mainack Mondal,Niloy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合词汇自适应与参数高效微调的领域适应方法，通过在预训练分词器中添加领域特定词元并选择性替换欠训练/不可达词元，在保持参数增长受限的同时提升LLM在专业领域（法律、医学）的文本摘要质量，训练时间减少35-55%，参数减少最高37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用域预训练的大语言模型在专业领域应用中存在分词效率低下和词汇不匹配问题，持续预训练虽能缓解性能下降但无法解决根本性的词汇表不匹配问题。

Method: 提出一种结合词汇自适应与参数高效微调的统一框架：(1) 向预训练分词器添加领域特定词元；(2) 选择性替换欠训练和不可达词元以限制参数增长；(3) 在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上评估，使用挑战导向的评估协议聚焦专家驱动文本中的高浓度OOV词。

Result: 词汇自适应算法提升了摘要的语义相似度、连贯性、相关性和忠实度；训练时间比持续预训练减少35-55%；参数数量比纯扩展方法减少37%。

Conclusion: 提出的词汇自适应与参数高效微调方法能有效解决领域词汇不匹配问题，在专业领域摘要任务上实现性能提升与资源效率的双重优势。

Abstract: Large language models pretrained on general-domain corpora often exhibit tokenization inefficiencies when applied to specialized domains. Although continual pretraining for domain adaptation partially alleviate performance degradation, it does not resolve the fundamental vocabulary mismatch. To address this gap, we introduce a targeted parameter-efficient domain adaptation approach that combines vocabulary adaptation with pretraining for LLM-based text summarization. Our unified framework augments pretrained tokenizers with domain-specific tokens while selectively replacing under-trained and unreachable tokens to limit parameter growth. We evaluate our approach on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B across legal and medical summarization tasks on a challenge-oriented evaluation protocol focused on expert-driven text and summaries which typically has higher concentration of over-fragmented Out-of-Vocabulary (OOV) words. The vocabulary adaptation algorithm enhances the overall quality of the summarization model by improving semantic similarity between the generated summaries and their references. In addition, the adapted model produces summaries that incorporate more appropriate novel and domain-specific words, leading to improved coherence, relevance, and faithfulness. We further observe that our proposed approach significantly reduce training time by $35-55\%$ over continual pretraining and reduce parameter counts up to $37\%$ w.r.t expansion-only methods. We make the codebase publicly available at https://github.com/gb-kgp/VocabReplace-Then-Expand.

</details>


### [41] [MiniGPT: Rebuilding GPT from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17398)
*Jibin Joseph*

Main category: cs.CL

Result: 0.83M参数基线模型在3000次训练迭代后验证损失为1.7236；10.77M参数强配置最佳验证损失为1.4780，生成文本具有可识别的莎士比亚风格对话结构。

Conclusion: MiniGPT未提出新的语言模型架构，而是记录了从原始文本到训练好字符级生成模型的清晰、可复现实现路径，包括设计选择、训练行为、生成质量和实际局限性。

Abstract: This paper presents MiniGPT, a compact from-scratch implementation of GPT-style autoregressive language modeling in PyTorch. The aim is to rebuild the core GPT pipeline from first principles after studying the design of nanoGPT by Andrej Karpathy, while keeping the model and training code independently written in a single notebook. MiniGPT implements token and positional embeddings, causal multi-head self-attention, pre-LayerNorm Transformer blocks, residual connections, feed-forward MLP layers, next-token cross-entropy training (teacher forcing), validation tracking, checkpoint selection, and autoregressive text generation. This paper evaluates the implementation on Tiny Shakespeare dataset using character-level tokenization. A baseline 0.83M-parameter model reaches a validation loss of 1.7236 after 3000 training iterations. A stronger 10.77M-parameter configuration, using a larger context length and improved training settings, reaches a best validation loss of 1.4780 and generates text with recognizable Shakespeare-style dialogue structure. MiniGPT does not introduce a new language-model architecture. Instead, it documents a clear and reproducible implementation path from raw text to trained character-level generation, including design choices, training behavior, generation quality, and practical limitations.

</details>


### [42] [BELIEF: Structured Evidence Modeling and Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17435)
*Chang Zong,Hao Ning,Siliang Tang,Jie Huang,Jian Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BELIEF框架，将检索文献转化为结构化证据对象，通过基于D-S理论的符号推理路径与LLM神经推理路径进行不确定性感知融合，在三个生物医学问答基准上取得25/30的最优结果，有效提升了证据利用的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强LLM方法将检索文献作为扁平文本输入模型，导致证据可靠性及决策不确定性隐式不显，缺乏对证据质量、来源可信度及候选答案支持强度的显式建模与融合机制。

Method: 首先将检索文档转化为结构化证据对象（记录临床属性、来源质量、问题相关性、支持强度及候选假设）；符号推理路径基于D-S理论构建可靠性加权基本概率分配，进行不确定性感知的符号证据融合；神经推理路径利用同一结构化证据进行LLM语义推断；可靠性感知仲裁模块根据信念强度、不确定性、证据可靠性和语义一致性调和符号与神经路径输出。

Result: 在PubMedQA、MedQA和MedMCQA三个基准上，使用五个通用LLM骨干，30个骨干-数据集-度量设置中获得25个最优结果；与生物医学领域模型相比，在MedQA和MedMCQA上具有竞争力，在PubMedQA上专用预训练仍具优势。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模证据结构、路径分歧和决策不确定性，BELIEF有效提升了检索证据的利用率，消融、互补性、不确定性分层及成本分析进一步验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Biomedical question answering often requires decisions from retrieved literature whose relevance, quality, and support for candidate answers are uneven. Most retrieval-augmented large language model (LLM) methods feed this literature to the model as flat text, leaving evidence reliability and remaining uncertainty largely implicit. We propose BELIEF, a structured evidence modeling and uncertainty-aware fusion framework for closed-set biomedical question answering. Rather than treating retrieved documents as undifferentiated context, BELIEF converts them into evidence objects that record clinical attributes, source quality, question relevance, support strength, and the associated candidate hypothesis. These evidence objects provide a shared basis for two complementary reasoning paths. The symbolic path constructs reliability-weighted basic probability assignments based on Dempster--Shafer (D-S) theory over a finite answer space and performs uncertainty-aware symbolic evidence fusion to estimate belief and residual uncertainty. The neural path uses the same structured evidence for LLM-based semantic inference, while a reliability-aware arbitration module reconciles the symbolic and neural outputs according to belief strength, uncertainty, evidence reliability, and semantic consistency. Experiments on PubMedQA, MedQA, and MedMCQA with five general-purpose LLM backbones show that BELIEF obtains the best result in 25 of 30 backbone--dataset--metric settings. Comparisons with biomedical-domain models indicate that BELIEF is competitive on MedQA and MedMCQA, while specialized biomedical pretraining remains advantageous on PubMedQA. Ablation, complementarity, uncertainty-stratified, and cost analyses further show that BELIEF improves retrieved-evidence utilization by making evidence structure, path disagreement, and decision uncertainty explicit.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Catalogue Counts: the Dataset Visibility Asymmetry in Low-Resource Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17442)
*Zhiyin Tan,Changxu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，多语言NLP中广泛使用的数据集目录（如LRE Map和LDC）严重低估了低资源语言的实际数据集活动。通过引入资源密度指数（RDI）和LLM辅助的引文挖掘管道，作者在141种目录中低可见度的语言中发现了609个数据集（其中356个仍可公开访问），表明语言数据稀缺不仅是生产问题，更是文档记录、可发现性和长期可访问性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言数据集目录（如LRE Map和LDC）只记录了已注册或机构分发的数据集，无法反映研究文献中实际被创建、引用或复用的数据集情况。这种目录可见性与实际研究活动之间的差距尚未被系统量化。

Method: 1) 基于目录（LRE Map和LDC）建立基线，计算资源密度指数RDI（每百万母语者对应的编录数据集数量），覆盖Ethnologue中200种使用最广泛的语言；2) 对141种低可见度语言（RDI<0.1），使用LLM辅助的引文挖掘管道从Semantic Scholar语料库中提取数据集引用；3) 人工验证和合并，检查数据集的可访问性。

Result: 在200种语言中，118种（59%）的RDI为0，23种低于0.1。在141种低可见度语言中，通过引文挖掘识别出53种语言共609个数据集，其中356个仍可通过公开链接访问。

Conclusion: 多语言数据稀缺不仅是生产问题，更是文档记录、可发现性和长期可访问性问题。许多大母语者数量的语言在目录中显得数据贫乏，但在研究文献中却存在明显的数据集活动。

Abstract: Multilingual NLP often relies on dataset counts from centralized catalogues to characterize which languages are resource-rich or resource-poor. However, these catalogues record only one layer of dataset visibility: what has been registered or institutionally distributed. They do not necessarily reflect which datasets are created, cited, or reused in the research literature. To examine this gap, we combine a catalogue-based baseline with literature-backed evidence of dataset circulation. We introduce the Resource Density Index (RDI), defined as the number of catalogued datasets per one million speakers, and compute it for the 200 most widely spoken languages in Ethnologue. Among them, 118 languages (59%) have an average RDI of zero across the LRE Map and the Linguistic Data Consortium (LDC), and another 23 fall below 0.1, corresponding to at most one catalogued dataset per ten million speakers. We then apply an LLM-assisted citation-mining pipeline over the Semantic Scholar corpus to these 141 low-visibility languages. After manual validation and consolidation, we identify 609 unique datasets across 53 languages, of which 356 remain openly accessible through working public links. These results reveal a substantial visibility gap: many large-speaker languages appear data-poor in catalogue records yet show clear evidence of dataset activity in the research literature. Our findings suggest that multilingual data scarcity should be understood not only as a production problem, but also as a question of documentation, discoverability, and long-term accessibility. Code and data are publicly available at (https://github.com/zhiyintan/dataset-visibility-asymmetry).

</details>


### [44] [Analyzing Error Propagation in Korean Spoken QA with ASR-LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17443)
*Donghyuk Jung,Youngwon Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了韩语口语问答中ASR错误通过ASR-LLM级联传播的问题，发现ASR错误导致的下游性能下降在不同LLM间保持一致，且韩语单字符ASR错误是独特的语义失败通道；同时，大型音频语言模型在噪声环境下优于匹配语言骨干的ASR-LLM流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估指标无法充分捕捉下游语义失败，因此需要分析ASR错误在ASR-LLM级联系统中如何传播并影响口语问答的语义理解。

Method: 通过ASR-LLM级联框架分析韩语SQA任务中ASR错误的下游影响，比较不同LLM的性能退化一致性，识别单字符ASR错误导致的语义失败，并与大型音频语言模型进行对比实验。

Result: ASR错误导致的下游性能退化在不同性能水平的LLM间一致；单字符韩语ASR错误会导致正确答案完全缺失；大型音频语言模型在噪声韩语SQA中优于ASR-LLM流水线。

Conclusion: ASR-LLM级联的性能退化主要由ASR阶段的信息损失决定，直接音频输入可缓解转录带来的信息损失，具有潜在优势。

Abstract: We analyze how automatic speech recognition (ASR) errors propagate through ASR-LLM cascades in Korean spoken question answering (SQA), focusing on downstream semantic failures that conventional ASR metrics cannot fully capture. Our analysis shows that the relative downstream degradation caused by ASR errors is consistent across LLMs with different absolute performance, suggesting that cascade degradation largely tracks ASR-stage information loss. We further identify single-character Korean ASR errors as a distinct semantic-failure channel, where the gold answer becomes entirely absent from the downstream prediction despite only a minimal transcription difference. Finally, an auxiliary comparison shows that a large audio language model outperforms an ASR-LLM pipeline with a matched language backbone in noisy Korean SQA, indicating the potential of direct audio input to mitigate transcript-induced information loss.

</details>


### [45] [VerifyMAS: Hypothesis Verification for Failure Attribution in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17467)
*Hezhe Qiao,Hanghang Tong,Ee-Peng Lim,Bing Liu,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VerifyMAS框架，通过假设验证方法替代直接预测，将故障归因分解为轨迹级错误验证和细粒度智能体定位，有效捕获全局故障模式并缩小搜索空间，在多个基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多智能体系统中不可靠的智能体是系统级可靠性的关键瓶颈。现有故障归因方法依赖局部日志，无法捕获跨步不一致性和智能体间协调错误等全局故障；且直接预测故障导致组合搜索空间过大，难以实现细粒度归因。

Method: 提出VerifyMAS假设验证框架：(1)将归因分解为轨迹级错误验证和细粒度智能体定位，采用错误优先的归因方式；(2)基于结构化错误分类法构建假设驱动的数据构造策略；(3)微调专用LLM验证器模型用于轨迹级故障验证和智能体归因。

Result: 在Aegis-Bench和Who&When基准测试上，VerifyMAS一致改进了包括开源Qwen和API-based GPT在内的多种骨干模型，优于先前方法，且不牺牲长多智能体轨迹的推理效率。

Conclusion: 基于假设验证的归因方法（错误优先、先验证后定位）比直接预测方法更有效，能够捕获全局故障模式并减小搜索空间，是提升LLM-MAS可靠性的可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model-driven multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) excel at complex tasks, yet unreliable agents remain a key bottleneck to system-level reliability. Automatic failure attribution is therefore critical, but existing approaches, such as direct prediction of agent-error pairs and agent-first failure attribution, rely on local logs of agents and miss global failures that only manifest over full interaction trajectories, such as cross-step inconsistencies and inter-agent coordination errors. Moreover, directly predicting failures induces a large combinatorial search space, hindering fine-grained attribution. To address these challenges, we propose VerifyMAS, a hypothesis verification framework for agent failure attribution. Instead of directly predicting faulty agents and error types, VerifyMAS formulates and verifies failure hypotheses against full trajectories. This verification-based approach decomposes attribution into trajectory-level error validation and fine-grained agent localization, providing an error-first attribution approach that captures global failure patterns while substantially reducing the search space. We further introduce a hypothesis-based data construction strategy grounded in a structured error taxonomy and fine-tune a specialized LLM verifier model for trajectory-level failure verification and agent attribution. Experiments on Aegis-Bench and Who&When show that VerifyMAS consistently improves diverse backbone models, including open-source Qwen and API-based GPT models, outperforming prior methods without sacrificing inference efficiency for long multi-agent trajectories.

</details>


### [46] [Hybrid Feature Combinations with CNN for Bangla Fake News Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17481)
*Md Gulzar Hussain,Babe Sultana,Md Rinku Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用CNN模型和特征选择方法（语义、统计、字符级特征及其组合），在BanFakeNews-2.0数据集上进行孟加拉语假新闻检测，发现组合特征比单一特征显著提升召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国人们日益依赖互联网和社交媒体获取新闻，但假新闻的传播对真实媒体的可信度构成威胁，而现有孟加拉语假新闻检测研究仍有改进空间。

Method: 使用CNN模型，探索语义、统计和字符级特征及其组合等多种特征选择方法，在BanFakeNews-2.0数据集上进行假新闻检测。

Result: 组合多种特征相比单独使用单一特征，显著提高了召回率和F1分数。

Conclusion: 特征组合策略能有效提升孟加拉语假新闻检测性能，代码已开源在GitHub上。

Abstract: Nowadays, people in Bangladesh frequently rely on the internet and social media for daily news instead of traditional newspapers. However, the spread of false Bangla news through these platforms poses risks and challenges to the credibility of authentic media. Although several studies have been conducted on detecting Bangla fake news, there is still significant room for improvement in this area. To assist people, this research explores the effectiveness of feature selection approaches in identifying appropriate features, such as semantic, statistical, and character-level features, or their combinations, on the BanFakeNews-2.0 dataset for detecting Bangla fake news using a CNN model. In this paper, key findings reveal that combining multiple features significantly improves recall and F1-scores compared to using individual features alone. The code for this research can be availed here, https://github.com/gulzar09/Bn\_FNews\_H.Feature.

</details>


### [47] [Residual Semantic Decomposition of Word Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17482)
*Seungmin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出残差语义分解（RSD）方法，通过对词嵌入进行神经加性分解，在重建嵌入与保持关系结构之间取得平衡。RSD支持递归二元分解，每个K=2的拟合提取一个局部语义轴，残差则揭示该轴未吸收的信息。在针对歧义词的手动指定配对语境诊断中，RSD能区分提供的语境锚点（优于打乱标签的对照），但熵诊断显示，歧义目标在静态GloVe中并非均匀的高熵边界点。因此，残差邻域应被视为定性诊断工具，而非基准语义预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词嵌入方法难以有效分离和解释嵌入空间中的多重语义信息，特别是对于歧义词的语义分解缺乏系统性的可解释方法。

Method: 提出残差语义分解（RSD）方法，采用神经加性分解对词嵌入进行递归二元分解，每个K=2的拟合提取局部语义轴，残差部分保留未被当前轴吸收的语义信息。

Result: 在歧义词的配对语境诊断中，RSD能够有效区分不同语境锚点，性能优于打乱标签的对照方法。但熵诊断表明歧义目标在静态GloVe中并非均匀的高熵边界点。

Conclusion: 残差邻域应作为定性诊断工具使用，而非用于基准语义预测任务。

Abstract: We introduce Residual Semantic Decomposition (RSD), a neural additive decomposition of word embeddings that balances embedding reconstruction with relational structure preservation. RSD supports recursive binary decomposition: each $K=2$ fit extracts a local semantic axis, while residuals expose information not absorbed by that axis. In manually specified paired-context diagnostics over ambiguous words, RSD separates supplied context anchors above shuffled-label controls, but entropy diagnostics show that ambiguous targets are not uniformly high-entropy boundary points in static GloVe. We therefore treat residual neighborhoods as qualitative diagnostics rather than benchmark sense predictions.

</details>


### [48] [Mixture of Experts for Low-Resource LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17598)
*Ori Bar Joseph,Smadar Arvatz,Noam Kayzer,Dan Revital,Sarel Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了MoE架构（Qwen3-30B-A3B和Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B）在低资源语言（希伯来语）上的路由动态，发现深层路由坍塌现象（后续层专家使用熵显著下降）。持续预训练（CPT）能有效修正该问题，而仅做SFT效果有限。日语实验证实该现象源于预训练数据代表性不足，而非语言固有属性。路由改善与下游任务性能提升相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽能高效扩展模型规模，但专家路由行为在低资源语言上的表现尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 使用希伯来语作为形态丰富的低资源测试平台，对比分析纯Transformer（Qwen3-30B-A3B）和混合Mamba-Transformer（Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B）两种架构的路由动态，并通过日语进行跨语言验证。采用持续预训练（CPT）和SFT两种校正策略。

Result: 两种模型在希伯来语上均表现出深层路由坍塌；CPT能显著修正路由不平衡，提升熵值并转向语言无关的共享专家；SFT效果有限；日语数据呈现一致的量化坍塌特征。

Conclusion: 路由坍塌是预训练代表性不足的系统性后果，而非语言固有属性；路由熵与专家专业化可作为MoE系统多语言能力的诊断指标。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient model scaling, yet expert routing behavior across underrepresented languages remains poorly understood. We analyze routing dynamics in two architecturally distinct MoE models -- a pure Transformer (Qwen3-30B-A3B) and a hybrid Mamba-Transformer (Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B) -- using Hebrew as a morphologically rich, low-resource testbed. Both pre-trained models exhibit \emph{deep-layer routing collapse}: usage entropy drops sharply in final layers and tokens concentrate on a narrow expert subset, a pattern largely absent for English. Continual pre-training (CPT) on balanced bilingual data substantially corrects this imbalance, increasing entropy and shifting routing toward shared, language-agnostic experts; supervised fine-tuning (SFT) alone achieves less complete correction. Extending the analysis to Japanese reveals quantitatively consistent collapse signatures, providing cross-linguistic evidence that the phenomenon is a systematic consequence of pre-training underrepresentation rather than any language-intrinsic property. Routing improvements correlate with consistent downstream benchmark gains, positioning routing entropy and expert specialization as principled diagnostics for multilingual capacity in MoE systems.

</details>


### [49] [Temporal Decay of Co-Citation Predictability: A 20-Year Statute Retrieval Benchmark from 396M Ukrainian Court Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17639)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建含396M引用的UA-StatuteRetrieval基准，发现在20年时间跨度下法律引文共现结构显著衰减（Adamic-Adar MRR下降33%-47%），且衰减呈现不均匀性：刑事程序法保持稳定，但民法因2017年司法改革MRR从0.35降至0.15。文本基线衰减更快（31%），嵌入漂移分析揭示了4.3%的语义偏移作为机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验法律信息系统中引文共现结构长期稳定的假设是否成立，并量化其时间衰减程度与分布特征。

Method: 构建UA-StatuteRetrieval基准（含乌克兰101M份判决的396M条法典引用，20年快照），采用留一法对二分引文图评估Adamic-Adar MRR，并对比固定文章集与训练/测试时间分割两种设置，结合BM25文本基线和E5-large嵌入漂移分析。

Result: Adamic-Adar MRR在固定文章集上从0.43降至0.29（下降33%），时间分割下从0.51降至0.27（下降47%）；刑法稳定（~0.40），民法从0.35降至0.15；中心文章（>100K引用）抗衰减，中频文章（1K-10K）预测性减半；BM25下降31%；E5-large嵌入漂移4.3%。

Conclusion: 引文共现结构并非长期稳定，其衰减依赖于法律领域和文章频次，司法改革是重要驱动因素，语义偏移提供了衰减的机制解释。

Abstract: Co-citation structure is widely assumed to provide stable retrieval signal in legal information systems. We test this assumption longitudinally by constructing UA-StatuteRetrieval, a benchmark that measures co-citation predictability across 20 annual snapshots (2007-2026) of 396 million codex citations from 101 million Ukrainian court decisions. Using a leave-one-out protocol over the full bipartite citation graph, we find that Adamic-Adar MRR declines 33% on a fixed set of articles (from 0.43 to 0.29) and 47% under a train/test temporal split (from 0.51 to 0.27) confirming genuine temporal decay rather than compositional shift or evaluation artifact. The decay is non-uniform: criminal procedure maintains stable co-citation patterns (MRR ~0.40), while civil law degrades from 0.35 to 0.15, coinciding with the 2017 judicial reform. Hub articles (>100K citations) resist decay, but mid-frequency articles (1K-10K) -- the practical retrieval frontier lose half their predictability. A BM25 text baseline decays even faster (31%), and embedding drift analysis with E5-large reveals a 4.3% semantic shift in how articles are cited, providing a mechanistic explanation for the observed decay. The benchmark is released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/overthelex/ua-statute-retrieval.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Transcripts: Iterative Peer-Editing with Audio Unlocks High-Quality Human Summaries of Conversational Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17652)
*Kaavya Chaparala,Thomas Thebaud,Jesús Villalba López,Laureano Moro-Velazquez,Peter Viechnicki,Najim Dehak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语音摘要任务中人工标注工作流的质量权衡，发现基于音频的摘要信息量较少且更压缩，但通过迭代同行编辑可以弥补这一差距，使其与基于文本的摘要和LLM摘要一样信息丰富。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音摘要任务缺乏成熟的基准数据集，而创建新基准需要人工标注，因为LLM可能将系统性错误和偏差引入数据集。需要探究不同标注工作流（输入模态、编辑方式）对摘要质量的影响。

Method: 测试了十种标注工作流，变化输入模态（音频、文本或两者）以及是否包含编辑（自我编辑或同行编辑）。比较人工音频摘要与人工文本摘要的质量差异，并将人工输出与四种LLM基准（三种文本、一种音频）进行对比。

Result: 音频摘要比文本摘要信息量更少、更压缩。但迭代同行编辑可缩小这一差距，使音频摘要与文本摘要及LLM摘要的信息量相当。

Conclusion: 迭代同行编辑验证了在无法获得文本转录时，利用词汇和韵律信息创建基准数据集的可行性，支持在无法获取转录文本的场景下进行关键数据集收集。

Abstract: There are not enough established benchmarks for the task fo speech summarization. Creating new benchmarks demands human annotation, as LLMs could embed systemic errors and bias into datasets. We test ten annotation workflows varying input modality (audio, transcript, or both) and the inclusion of editing (self or peer-editing) to investigate potential quality tradeoffs from using human annotators to summarize audio. We compare human audio-based summaries to human transcript-based summaries to track the impact of the different information modalities on summary quality. We also compare the human outputs against four LLM benchmarks (three text, one audio) to examine whether human-written summaries are less informative than highly fluent automated outputs. We find that audio-based summaries are less informative and more compressed than transcript summaries. However, iterative peer-editing with audio mitigates this difference, enabling audio-based summaries to be as informative as their transcript counterparts and LLM summaries. These findings validate iterative peer-editing among human annotators for the creation of benchmarks informed by both lexical and prosodic information. This enables crucial dataset collection even in setting where transcripts are unavailable.

</details>


### [51] [Stop When Reasoning Converges: Semantic-Preserving Early Exit for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17672)
*Dehai Min,Giovanni Vaccarino,Huiyi Chen,Yongliang Wu,Gal Yona,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PUMA是一种即插即用的框架，通过轻量级冗余检测器识别推理步骤中的语义冗余，结合答案级验证实现早期退出，在不影响答案准确性和推理链完整性的前提下，平均减少26.2%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）在生成长思维链时存在"过度思考"问题，即解决方案已稳定后仍继续推理，浪费token并增加延迟。现有的早期退出方法主要依赖答案级信号（如置信度），这些信号反映答案就绪状态而非推理收敛性，可能导致过早退出，损害最终答案准确性并使推理链语义不完整。

Method: 提出PUMA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级冗余检测器（Redundancy Detector），用于识别推理步骤中语义冗余的候选退出点；(2) 答案级验证机制，确认停止是否安全。两者结合，在移除冗余继续推理的同时保留答案准确性和连贯的推理前缀。

Result: 在5种LRM和5个具有挑战性的推理基准上，PUMA平均减少26.2%的token消耗，同时保持准确率和保留的思维链质量。在代码生成、零样本视觉-语言推理和学习停止策略内化等额外实验中，证明了推理级冗余是一种鲁棒、可迁移且可学习的有效推理信号。

Conclusion: 推理层面的语义冗余是互补于答案级信号的早期退出信号，当连续推理步骤不再增加新进展而重复已有结论时，推理轨迹可能已收敛。PUMA证明了这种信号的有效性、可迁移性和可学习性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance by generating long chains of thought (CoT), but often overthink, continuing to reason after a solution has already stabilized and thereby wasting tokens and increasing latency. Existing inference-time early-exit methods rely primarily on answer-level signals, such as confidence or trial-answer consistency, to decide when to stop. However, these signals mainly reflect answer readiness rather than reasoning convergence: they may trigger before the model has finished exploring or self-correcting, causing premature exits that can degrade final-answer accuracy and leave the retained reasoning chain semantically incomplete. We identify reasoning-level semantic redundancy as a complementary signal for semantic-preserving early exit: when successive steps no longer add novel progress and instead revisit established conclusions, the reasoning trajectory has likely converged. Building on this insight, we propose PUMA, a plug-and-play framework that combines a lightweight Redundancy Detector with answer-level verification. The detector flags semantically redundant candidate exits, while verification confirms whether stopping is safe, allowing PUMA to remove redundant continuation while preserving both answer accuracy and a coherent reasoning prefix. Across five LRMs and five challenging reasoning benchmarks, PUMA achieves 26.2% average token reduction while preserving accuracy and retained CoT quality. Additional experiments on code generation, zero-shot vision-language reasoning, and learned stopping-policy internalization further demonstrate that reasoning-level redundancy is a robust, transferable, and learnable signal for efficient reasoning. Our code is available at \url{https://github.com/giovanni-vaccarino/PUMA}.

</details>


### [52] [Validate Your Authority: Benchmarking LLMs on Multi-Label Precedent Treatment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17691)
*M. Mikail Demir,M. Abdullah Canbaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对法律先例中负面处理分类这一高风险NLP任务，提出了新的评估框架、专家标注数据集（239个真实法律引用）和平均严重性误差（Average Severity Error）指标。实验表明，Gemini 2.5 Flash在高层次分类任务上准确率最高（79.1%），GPT-5-mini在细粒度分类上表现最佳（67.7%），为复杂法律推理任务建立了关键基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律先例中负面处理的自动化分类具有高风险性，而标准准确率指标存在缺陷，无法有效衡量分类错误的实际影响，因此需要更鲁棒的评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个由专家标注的239个真实法律引用数据集，在现代大型语言模型上进行基准测试，并提出了新颖的平均严重性误差（Average Severity Error）指标来衡量分类错误的实际影响。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash在高层次分类任务中准确率达79.1%，GPT-5-mini在复杂细粒度分类中准确率达67.7%，模型在不同粒度任务上表现存在性能分化。

Conclusion: 该工作为法律推理任务建立了关键基准，提供了富含上下文的新数据集，并引入了针对该复杂任务需求量身定制的评估指标。

Abstract: Automating the classification of negative treatment in legal precedent is a critical yet nuanced NLP task where misclassification carries significant risk. To address the shortcomings of standard accuracy, this paper introduces a more robust evaluation framework. We benchmark modern Large Language Models on a new, expert-annotated dataset of 239 real-world legal citations and propose a novel Average Severity Error metric to better measure the practical impact of classification errors. Our experiments reveal a performance split. Google's Gemini 2.5 Flash achieved the highest accuracy on a high-level classification task (79.1%), while OpenAI's GPT-5-mini was the top performer on the more complex fine-grained schema (67.7%). This work establishes a crucial baseline, provides a new context-rich dataset, and introduces an evaluation metric tailored to the demands of this complex legal reasoning task.

</details>


### [53] [Do LLM Agents Mirror Socio-Cognitive Effects in Power-Asymmetric Conversations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17694)
*Anvesh Rao Vijjini,Sagar Manjunath,Snigdha Chaturvedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在模拟高低权力身份时，是否表现出与人类类似的权力社会认知效应，包括语言协调、代词使用、说服成功率和服从不安全指令四个方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在权力不对称对话中是否复现人类的社会认知模式，以及这种模拟是否会导致安全风险。

Method: 采用不同职业身份角色，模拟多轮权力不对称对话（如校长-教师、法官-律师），测量语言协调、代词使用、说服成功率和服从不安全请求四个指标。

Result: LLM表现出权力相关的社会认知效应，尽管存在细微差异和变异性。

Conclusion: 模拟的权力互动既会产生期望行为，也会导致不安全行为，提示需要关注LLM角色扮演中的安全风险。

Abstract: Power differences shape human communication through well documented socio cognitive effects, including language coordination, pronoun usage, authority bias, and harmful compliance. We examine whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit similar behaviors when assigned high or low status personas. Using personas from diverse professions, we simulate multi turn, power asymmetric dialogues (e.g., principal teacher, justice lawyer) and measure (i) linguistic coordination, (ii) pronoun usage, (iii) persuasion success, and (iv) compliance with unsafe requests. Our results show that LLMs show key socio cognitive effects of power, albeit with nuances and variability, linking simulated interactions to both desirable and unsafe behaviors.

</details>


### [54] [Sometin Beta Pass Notin (SBPN): Improving Multilingual ASR for Nigerian Languages via Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17710)
*Sewade Ogun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段蒸馏（知识蒸馏+伪标签自迭代）构建多语言ASR框架SBPN，覆盖约鲁巴语、豪萨语、伊博语、尼日利亚皮钦语和尼日利亚英语，在Common Voice和Fleurs上平均WER相对降低29%，超越单语及现有最佳多语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼日利亚语言在ASR系统中性能显著落后于英语、法语等高资源语言，面临数据稀缺、正字法不一致、声调符号、口音多样、频繁语码转换和本地化命名实体等独特建模难题。

Method: 采用两阶段蒸馏流程：第一阶段利用学生-教师知识蒸馏，基于现有单语模型并以语言特定N-gram语言模型为条件；第二阶段使用伪标注数据迭代自改进以进一步提升精度。

Result: 相比单语基线，平均相对WER降低29%；在Common Voice和Fleurs等主要基准上超越现有最佳多语言模型。发布SBPN-Base（1.2亿参数）和SBPN-Large（6亿参数）两种规模。

Conclusion: SBPN有效缩小了尼日利亚语言与高资源语言之间的性能差距，作为开放基础模型发布，为区域丰富的语音和文化景观研究提供ASR资源。

Abstract: Although modern multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems support several Nigerian languages, their performance consistently lags behind high-resource languages like English and French. Nigerian languages present unique modelling hurdles, including acute data scarcity, inconsistent orthography, tonal diacritics, diverse accents, frequent code-switching, and localized named entities. To address these challenges, we developed a multilingual ASR framework utilizing a two-stage distillation process. First, we employ student-teacher knowledge distillation from existing monolingual models, conditioned on robust language-specific N-gram language models. Second, we perform iterative self improvement using pseudo-labelled data to further refine accuracy. Our method significantly bridges the performance gap, achieving on average a relative Word Error Rate (WER) reduction of 29 % over monolingual baselines. Our models also outperform state-of-the-art multilingual models across major benchmarks, including Common Voice and Fleurs. We introduce Sometin Beta Pass Notin (SBPN), a foundational multilingual ASR model covering Yorùbá, Hausa, Igbo, Nigerian Pidgin, and Nigerian English. SBPN is released in two sizes: SBPN-Base (120 M parameters) and SBPN-Large (600 M parameters). By releasing these as open foundation models, we aim to provide ASR resources for further research into the rich phonetic and cultural landscape of the region.

</details>


### [55] [Bridging the Version Gap: Multi-version Training Improves ICD Code Prediction, Especially for Rare Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17755)
*Jinghui Liu,Anthony Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过结合不同ICD版本（ICD-9和ICD-10）的标注数据来训练版本无关的临床编码模型，发现多版本训练能够显著提升罕见代码的预测性能（稀有ICD-10代码的micro F1提升27%），同时改善高频代码的宏平均指标，且参数量更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICD编码是耗时耗力的管理任务，现有模型通常针对特定ICD版本优化，但ICD系统持续演进且不同版本在不同时期和地区使用；同时存在长尾分布问题，罕见代码性能是模型落地的瓶颈。作者希望探索通过融合多版本ICD标注数据来训练版本无关的模型，以解决上述挑战。

Method: 采用改进的label-wise注意力模型，将ICD-9数据加入ICD-10预测任务的训练中，构建版本无关的多版本训练框架。

Result: 相比仅使用ICD-10训练，加入ICD-9数据使18K个稀有ICD-10代码的micro F1提升27%；对8K个高频ICD-10代码，多版本训练显著改善宏平均指标，且模型参数量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 多版本联合训练是可行的，能够有效缓解ICD编码的版本迁移和长尾分布问题，为开发真正可落地的临床编码自动化模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Clinical coding maps clinical documentation to standardized medical codes, an essential yet time-consuming administrative task that could benefit from automation. Current models on ICD coding are typically optimized for codes from a specific ICD version. However, in reality, ICD systems evolve continuously, and different versions are adopted across time periods and regions. Moreover, ICD coding suffers from the long-tail problem, and rare code performance can be a bottleneck for developing implementable models. We examine whether it is viable to train version-independent models by combining data annotated in different ICD versions, which may help address these challenges. We add ICD-9 data to the training of a modified label-wise attention model for ICD-10 prediction, and find that despite the version mismatch, adding ICD-9 yields a 27% increase in micro F1 for 18K rare ICD codes compared to training on ICD-10 alone. On 8K frequent ICD-10 codes, the multi-version training also substantially improves macro metrics, with far fewer model parameters.

</details>


### [56] [Internalizing Tool Knowledge in Small Language Models via QLoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17774)
*Yuval Shemla,Ayal Yakobe,Tanmay Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过QLoRA参数高效微调将工具使用知识内化到小语言模型中，使模型在推理时无需显式工具描述即可进行结构化规划。在AssetOpsBench上微调Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3-4B后，模型在无描述推理设置下输入长度减少82.6%，规划性能超越接收完整工具描述的非微调基线，且Qwen3-4B在内存效率上更优但存在灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用管线需要在每次提示中包含完整工具模式，造成大量token开销，限制了小模型的实用性。本文探究能否通过参数高效微调将工具使用知识内化到小语言模型权重中，从而在推理时省略显式工具描述，降低推理开销。

Method: 使用AssetOpsBench基准，采用8-bit QLoRA对Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3-4B进行微调，训练数据包含约1,700个工具使用样本，涵盖工具知识、问题到规划映射和执行风格轨迹。在无描述推理设置下评估，同时进行LoRA秩的消融实验。

Result: 微调模型在输入长度减少82.6%的同时，规划得分显著提升：最佳Gemma运行的AT-F1为0.65、整体评判得分3.88，而接收完整工具描述的基线分别为0.47和2.88。Qwen3-4B整体评判得分3.78，内存使用减少62%、速度提升2.5倍，但在通用多选题基准上表现出更大的灾难性遗忘。LoRA秩r=32最大化规划质量，更小秩保留更多通用知识。

Conclusion: 对于固定工具目录，QLoRA微调可以将工具知识从提示上下文转移到模型权重中，在维持或提升工具规划质量的同时大幅降低推理开销。LoRA秩控制质量与知识保留之间的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as planning components in agentic systems, but current tool-use pipelines often require full tool schemas to be included in every prompt, creating substantial token overhead and limiting the practicality of smaller models. This paper investigates whether tool-use knowledge can be internalized into small language models through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, enabling structured planning without explicit tool descriptions at inference time. Using AssetOpsBench as the primary benchmark, we fine-tune Gemma 4 E4B and Qwen3-4B with 8-bit QLoRA on approximately 1,700 tool-use examples spanning tool knowledge, question-to-plan mappings, and execution-style traces. We evaluate the resulting models under description-free inference, where the prompt omits the tool catalog entirely. The fine-tuned models outperform an informed unfine-tuned baseline that receives full tool descriptions, reducing input length by 82.6\% while improving structural and LLM-judge planning scores. In the best Gemma run, the model achieves an AT-F1 of 0.65 and an overall judge score of 3.88, compared with 0.47 and 2.88 for the informed baseline. Qwen3-4B achieves a strong overall judge score of 3.78 while using 62\% less memory and running 2.5$\times$ faster than Gemma, though it also exhibits greater catastrophic forgetting on general multiple-choice benchmarks. Additional ablations show that LoRA rank controls a quality--retention trade-off, with $r=32$ maximizing planning quality and smaller ranks preserving more general knowledge. These results suggest that, for fixed tool catalogs, QLoRA fine-tuning can shift tool knowledge from prompt context into model weights, substantially reducing inference overhead while maintaining or improving tool-planning quality.

</details>


### [57] [Systematic Evaluation of the Quality of Synthetic Clinical Notes Rephrased by LLMs at Million-Note Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17775)
*Jinghui Liu,Sarvesh Soni,Anthony Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对LLM生成的临床文本进行了系统性评估，涵盖内在、外在和事实性评估。研究发现合成笔记保留了粗粒度任务的临床信息和预测效用，但丢失了细粒度细节（如ICD编码）。通过分块改写可减轻细节丢失，但会降低事实精度。错误分析发现合成错误主要源于临床上下文误解、时间混淆、测量错误和捏造。合成笔记虽为任务无关，但能有效增强稀有ICD代码的任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常只关注单一维度（如相似性或效用比较），缺乏对LLM生成临床文本的多维度系统性评估。本研究旨在通过内在、外在和事实性评估的多视角平行分析，全面评估LLM合成临床文本的质量。

Method: 基于MIMIC数据库，在百万笔记规模上对LLM重述的合成临床笔记进行系统性评估，包括内在评估（语言质量）、外在评估（预测效用）和事实性评估（事实核查与错误分析）。比较整体改写与分块改写两种策略，并评估合成笔记在稀有ICD编码任务上的数据增强效果。

Result: 合成笔记保留了核心临床信息和粗粒度任务的预测效用，但丢失了细粒度细节（如ICD编码）。分块改写可显著缓解细节丢失，但会导致事实精度下降。错误主要由临床上下文误解、时间混淆、测量误差和捏造组成。合成笔记能有效增强稀有ICD编码的训练。

Conclusion: LLM生成的临床文本在粗粒度任务上表现良好，但细粒度任务仍需谨慎；分块改写是平衡细节与事实精度的可行策略；合成数据虽为任务无关生成，但对稀有编码任务具有数据增强价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate or synthesize clinical text for a wide range of applications, from improving clinical documentation to augmenting clinical text analytics. Yet evaluations typically focus on a narrow aspect -- such as similarity or utility comparisons -- even though these aspects are complementary and best viewed in parallel. In this study, we aim to conduct a systematic evaluation of LLM-generated clinical text, which includes intrinsic, extrinsic, and factuality evaluations of synthetic clinical notes rephrased from MIMIC databases at million-note scale. Our analysis demonstrates that synthetic notes preserve core clinical information and predictive utility for coarse-grained tasks despite substantial linguistic changes, but lose fine-grained details for task like ICD coding. We show this loss of detail can be substantially mitigated by rephrasing notes by chunks rather than by the whole note, but at the cost of reduced factual precision under incomplete context. Through fact-checking and error analysis, we further find that synthesis errors are dominated by misinterpretation of clinical context, alongside temporal confusion, measurement errors, and fabricated claims. Finally, we show that the synthetic notes -- despite their task-agnostic nature -- can effectively augment task-specific training for rare ICD codes.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [58] [From Prompts to Protocols: An AI Agent for Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16552)
*Angelos Angelopoulos,James F. Cahoon,Ron Alterovitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种集成大语言模型的AI智能体架构，用于自动化科学实验室，支持自然语言交互式创建、监控和纠错实验协议，在三类模拟实验室中实现97%的首轮协议生成成功率和数量级的操作简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化实验室需要协调大量仪器和机器人，科学家被迫编写代码、管理配置文件并处理复杂的软件基础设施，缺乏直观的自然语言交互方式。

Method: 将大语言模型与实验编排系统（EOS）集成，构建agentic loop机制实现自动验证与错误修正，并设计可视化图形编辑器实现AI辅助与手动构建的无缝切换。

Result: 在化学、生物学、材料科学三个模拟自动化实验室中，AI智能体实现了97%的首轮协议生成成功率，所需的界面操作数量减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该架构有效降低了自动化实验室的配置门槛，使科学家能够通过自然语言高效创建和管理实验流程，显著提升实验自动化效率。

Abstract: Automating science laboratories enables faster, safer, more accurate, and more reproducible execution of protocols, accelerating the discovery and testing of new materials, drugs, and more. However, setting up and running autonomous labs requires coordinating numerous instruments and robots, forcing scientists to write code, manage configuration files, and navigate complex software infrastructure. We present an AI agent architecture that integrates large language models with laboratory orchestration, enabling scientists to interactively create and monitor automated lab protocols using natural language. Integrated into the Experiment Orchestration System (EOS), the AI agent operates under an agentic loop with automated validation and error correction, and supports the complete experimental lifecycle: creating protocols, running and monitoring both protocols and closed-loop optimization campaigns, and analyzing results. A visual graph editor renders protocols as interactive node-based diagrams synchronized with the AI agent's protocol representation, enabling seamless alternation between AI-assisted and manual protocol construction. Evaluated on three simulated automated labs spanning chemistry, biology, and materials science, the AI agent achieves a 97% first-attempt protocol generation success rate and an order of magnitude reduction in required interface actions.

</details>


### [59] [Scalable Uncertainty Reasoning in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16568)
*Jingcheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出一个模块化框架，通过代数、逻辑和几何三种专门化推理机制，分别处理知识图谱中属性值不精确、三元组存在概率不确定和模式知识不完整三个层次的不确定性，旨在实现语义精度与计算可处理性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱中的现实数据通常具有内在不确定性，但现有语义网标准缺乏对这种不确定性的原生支持，而朴素的扩展方法往往导致计算不可处理。

Method: 提出模块化框架，包括：(1)定义概率字面量和连续属性查询代数；(2)基于编译的方法将SPARQL溯源转换为可处理的概率电路；(3)拓扑感知的几何嵌入用于统计模式推理。

Result: 通过代数、逻辑和几何三种专门化推理机制，在语义精度与计算可处理性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 专门化的推理机制（代数、逻辑、几何方法）能够调和语义精度与计算可处理性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs are pivotal for semantic data integration. The real-world data they model is often inherently uncertain. Within knowledge graphs, uncertainty manifests in three distinct levels: imprecise attribute values, probabilistic triple existence, and incomplete schema knowledge. However, current Semantic Web standards lack native support for reasoning over such uncertainty, and naïve extensions often incur computational intractability. In this thesis, I aim to develop a modular framework that addresses each level through tailored techniques: (1) defining probabilistic literals and a corresponding query algebra for continuous attributes; (2) a compilation-based framework transforming SPARQL provenance into tractable probabilistic circuits for uncertain triples; and (3) topology-aware geometric embeddings for statistical schema reasoning. The central hypothesis is that specialized reasoning mechanisms, namely algebraic, logical, and geometric approaches, can reconcile semantic precision with computational tractability.

</details>


### [60] [Counterparty Modeling is Not Strategy: The Limits of LLM Negotiators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16575)
*Romain Cosentino,Sarath Shekkizhar,Adam Earle,Silvio Savarese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前LLM智能体能够建模对手偏好，但无法将这种知识转化为策略性谈判优势。它们在推理中早期就能准确建模对方偏好，却未能借此在自身高价值属性上获利。最终协议主要由表面锚定效应而非效用权重决定。要求智能体明确陈述让步-互惠交易虽使单轮看起来更具策略性，但未能提升最终协议效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谈判不仅需要推断对方意图，还需要在多轮博弈中利用这些信息做出有利的出价和还价。研究旨在检验LLM智能体是否能在多属性议价环境中做到这一点。

Method: 在可控的多属性议价环境中，研究LLM智能体的谈判行为：1) 测试其对对手偏好的建模能力；2) 分析其是否将偏好知识转化为策略性谈判；3) 进行回合级分析；4) 测试要求智能体明确陈述让步-互惠交易的干预措施。

Result: LLM能准确建模对手偏好，但未可靠地转化为策略优势；知情方常做出补偿不足的让步；卖家整体更迁就；最终协议由表面锚定效应决定而非效用权重；要求明确陈述让步-互惠交易未能改善最终协议效率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在谈判中缺乏真正策略性利用效用结构的能力，其行为更多受表面因素驱动，而非内在的博弈优化。

Abstract: Negotiation requires more than inferring what the other side wants: it requires using that information to make advantageous offers and counteroffers over multiple turns. We study whether large language model (LLM) agents do this in a controlled multi-attribute bargaining environment. We find that current LLM agents can model a counterparty's preferences, but do not reliably turn that knowledge into strategic bargaining. When given negotiating partner preference information, agents model it accurately and early in their reasoning traces, yet this does not reliably improve outcomes for the informed side. Turn-level analyses show why: agents often respond to what they believe the counterparty values, but do not consistently pair those moves with gains on their own high-value attributes. Sellers are more accommodating overall, and in asymmetric-information conditions, the informed side often makes the more weakly compensated concessions. Because agents fail to leverage this underlying utility structure for strategic advantage, their final agreements are heavily dictated by surface-level opening anchors rather than actual utility weights. Finally, requiring agents to explicitly state concession-for-reciprocity trades before making an offer makes individual turns look more strategic, but ultimately fails to improve the efficiency of the final agreements.

</details>


### [61] [TTE-Flash: Accelerating Reasoning-based Multimodal Representations via Think-Then-Embed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16638)
*Jianpeng Cheng,Xian Wu,Jiangfan Zhang,Wentao Bao,Chaitanya Ahuja,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Hanchao Yu,Yang Gao,Fan Xia,Qi Guo,Shaodan Zhai,Xiangjun Fan,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用潜在思考令牌（latent think tokens）替代显式思维链（CoT）来生成推理感知的多模态嵌入，在保持推理性能的同时降低计算成本。提出的TTE-Flash-2B模型在MMEB-v2基准上超越显式CoT方法，且潜在思考令牌具有可解释性，并在视频零样本评估中展示了扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入（UME）从思维链（CoT）推理中获益显著，但显式CoT推理的计算开销过大，需要一种更高效的方式来生成推理感知的表示。

Method: 提出用潜在思考令牌替代显式CoT推理，将思考令牌视为潜在变量，显式CoT推理视为观测变量。通过CoT生成损失优化思考令牌，再通过对比学习优化后续的嵌入令牌，从而在恒定推理成本下产生推理感知表示。研究两种架构设计：1）思考令牌和嵌入令牌如何从同一LLM主干中提取；2）两个依赖任务如何联合训练。

Result: TTE-Flash-2B在MMEB-v2基准上超越其显式CoT对应模型；15个视频数据集的零样本评估显示，随着思考令牌数量增加呈现扩展行为；潜在思考令牌在文本和视觉上均具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 潜在思考令牌可以替代显式CoT推理生成高性能的推理感知多模态嵌入，且计算成本恒定。思考令牌数量存在扩展行为，可基于任务需求进行自适应思考预算分配。

Abstract: Recent research has demonstrated that Universal Multimodal Embedding (UME) benefits significantly from Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. In this paradigm, a generative model produces explicit reasoning traces for a multimodal query, with the final representation extracted from an <eos> embedding token attending to both the query and the reasoning. Despite its effectiveness, the computational overhead of generating explicit CoT traces is often prohibitive. In this work, we propose replacing explicit CoT with latent think tokens, which are interpreted as latent variables that can produce explicit CoT traces as observed variables. By optimizing think tokens using CoT generation loss and subsequent embedding tokens using contrastive loss, we produce high-performance, reasoning-aware representations at a constant inference cost. Our study investigates two key architectural designs: 1) how think and embeddings tokens should be extracted from the same LLM backbone. 2) how the tokens should be trained as two dependent tasks. We introduce TTE-Flash-2B, a reasoning-aware multimodal representation model that outperforms its explicit-CoT counterpart on the MMEB-v2 benchmark, while producing latent think tokens that are interpretable both textually and visually. Furthermore, zero-shot evaluation across 15 video datasets reveals scaling behavior as the number of think tokens increases, and motivating a pilot study of adaptive think budget allocation based on task requirements.

</details>


### [62] [LinAlg-Bench: A Forensic Benchmark Revealing Structural Failure Modes in LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16675)
*Shradha Agarwal,Deepak Rajbhar,Tariq J*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinAlg-Bench是一个诊断线性代数计算的基准，测试10个前沿大语言模型在3x3、4x4和5x5矩阵上的结构化计算能力。通过对660个SymPy认证的问题和6600个模型输出进行分析，引入三阶段自动化取证管线将1156个失败分类为10种错误类型，发现大语言模型的数学失败不是随机的，而是由算法类型和矩阵维度结构性地约束。核心发现是在4x4尺度存在尖锐行为阈值：低于该阈值时模型出现执行错误（符号跟踪失败、算术漂移、奇偶错误），高于该阈值时失败转变为计算放弃，模型通过工具角色扮演、约束一致虚构和结构化幻觉来编造响应。这种从编造到放弃的转变在几乎所有模型层级和架构中普遍存在，表明是工作记忆限制而非知识差距，并由三种尺度涌现错误类型支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在数学推理任务上的评估主要关注最终答案的正确性（二元准确率），但缺乏对失败模式的细粒度分析。研究者希望系统性地理解LLM在结构化线性代数计算中失败的本质，特别是失败是否随机分布，还是受算法类型和问题规模的结构性约束。

Method: 构建LinAlg-Bench基准，包含9种任务类型、660个SymPy认证的问题，覆盖3x3、4x4、5x5三个维度梯度。评估10个前沿大语言模型，共6600个模型输出。除二元准确率外，引入三阶段自动化取证管线，将1156个失败分类为10个主要错误标签及细分子类型。分析错误分布与矩阵维度、算法类型、模型层级之间的关系。

Result: 核心发现是4x4尺度存在尖锐行为阈值：3x3时模型主要出现执行错误（符号跟踪失败、算术漂移、奇偶错误）；4x4和5x5时失败转变为计算放弃，模型通过工具角色扮演、约束一致虚构和结构化幻觉来编造响应。该转变在几乎所有模型层级和架构中普遍存在。发现三种尺度涌现错误类型（仅出现在4x4和5x5而不出现在3x3）。还发现解决方案策略刚性是5x5行列式准确率的近乎完美预测器，并识别出约束意识虚构作为一种新型结构化幻觉失败模式。

Conclusion: LLM在结构化线性代数计算中的失败不是随机或知识缺口所致，而是受工作记忆限制的结构性现象。从编造到放弃的转变表明模型在超出其有效工作记忆范围时会从试图计算转向编造合理但错误的答案。研究提供了细粒度错误分类学、诊断工具和公开数据集，为未来改进LLM数学推理能力提供方向。

Abstract: We introduce LinAlg-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark evaluating 10 frontier large language models on structured linear algebra computation across a strict dimensional gradient of 3x3, 4x4, and 5x5 matrices. Spanning 9 task types and 660 SymPy-certified problems, the benchmark exhaustively evaluates 6,600 model outputs. Beyond binary accuracy, LinAlg-Bench introduces a three-stage automated forensic pipeline classifying 1,156 failures into ten primary error tags with fine-grained subtypes, revealing that LLM mathematical failure is not random but structurally constrained by algorithm type and matrix dimension. Our central finding is a sharp behavioral threshold at 4x4 scale: below it, models fail through execution errors -- sign tracking failures, arithmetic drift, and parity errors; above it, failure transitions to computational abandonment, with models fabricating responses through tool roleplay, constraint-consistent confabulation, and structured hallucination rather than attempting computation. This fabrication-to-abandonment transition is near-universal across all model tiers and architectures, suggesting a working memory limit rather than a knowledge gap, supported by three scale-emergent error types absent at 3x3 but present at 4x4 and 5x5. We further show that solution strategy rigidity is a near-perfect predictor of 5x5 determinant accuracy, document constraint-aware confabulation as a novel structured hallucination failure mode, and release all data, model outputs, error labels, and judge pipeline publicly.

</details>


### [63] [Recall Isn't Enough: Bounding Commitments in Personalized Language Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16712)
*Rui Tang,Yichi Zhang,Xi Chen,Chen Dong,Youwei Yang,Yumeng Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CBEA+LCV方法，通过有界证据激活和承诺验证，在长上下文中实现零失败，但仅召回0.012的可见事实，以74-75%更低的输入负载换取可控的承诺边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文和记忆系统将个性化视为回忆问题，但实际故障常发生在系统做出承诺时（如将噪声转化为硬约束、丢弃罕见证据、遗忘后续义务等），缺乏对承诺可靠性的保障机制。

Method: 提出Contract-Bounded Evidence Activation (CBEA) 和 Lexicographic Commitment Validation (LCV)。CBEA通过类型化覆盖、尾部证据和后果债务激活有界证据集；LCV在生成文本前验证结构化承诺，将不可行状态路由到修复、弃权或重新缔约。

Result: 在360个测试用例和三个生成后端上，CBEA+LCV在验证器范围内达到零失败，可用性0.49-0.60；原始和长上下文基线仅达0.003-0.092。阴影预言机诊断显示CBEA+LCV仅召回0.012的未编译可见事实，而原始召回0.53。

Conclusion: CBEA+LCV实现了一个有界操作点：显式承诺控制和74-75%更低的中间输入负载，而非通用记忆优势。

Abstract: Long-context and memory systems usually treat personalization as a recall problem. In practice, many failures occur later, when a system commits: it turns noisy hints into hard constraints, drops rare witnesses, forgets downstream obligations, or answers despite infeasibility. We introduce Contract-Bounded Evidence Activation (CBEA) with Lexicographic Commitment Validation (LCV). CBEA activates a bounded evidence set using typed coverage, tail witnesses, and consequence debt; LCV validates structured commitments before prose and routes infeasible states to repair, abstention, or recontract. Across 360 fixtures and three generation backends, CBEA+LCV reaches zero failures within validator scope at 0.49-0.60 availability over attempted runs. Raw and long-context baselines with the same LCV gate reach zero only at 0.003-0.092. A shadow oracle diagnostic marks the limit: CBEA+LCV recalls 0.012 of uncompiled visible facts, while raw recalls 0.53. The result is a bounded operating point: explicit commitment control and 74-75% lower median input payload, not universal memory dominance.

</details>


### [64] [Baba in Wonderland: Online Self-Supervised Dynamics Discovery for Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16725)
*SeungWon Seo,DongHeun Han,SeongRae Noh,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Alice系统，通过在先验错配条件下利用候选更新失败的结构信号（保留冲突）来学习可执行世界模型，并借助假设类细化和类感知探索提升学习效果，在Baba in Wonderland环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可执行世界模型学习中，当先验知识（如语义标签）与真实环境动态不一致时（即先验错配），agent无法依赖规则描述、奖励信号或可信词汇先验，必须仅从交互证据中归纳状态依赖的动态规律，现有方法在此条件下表现不佳。

Method: 提出Alice闭环系统：(1) 将失败的候选更新视为结构信号，利用保留冲突（新候选解释新转换但丢失已解释转换）揭示当前程序混淆的动态；(2) 将这些冲突精炼为假设类（hypothesis classes），提供紧凑的、按类分层的保留反例用于更新；(3) 引导前沿探索（frontier exploration）朝向当前程序中新颖且代表性不足的转换。

Result: 在Baba in Wonderland（先验错配版Baba Is You）上的实验表明，Alice显著提升了先验错配条件下可执行世界模型的学习性能；消融实验证实，类细化和类感知探索两者均为性能提升的关键贡献因素。

Conclusion: 利用候选更新失败的结构信号进行冲突精炼和类感知探索，能够有效应对先验错配下的可执行世界模型学习问题，为缺乏可靠先验知识的在线环境动态建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Executable world models can be read, edited, executed, and reused for planning, but only if the program captures the environment's transition law rather than semantic shortcuts in its surface vocabulary. We study online executable world-model learning under prior misalignment, where an agent must induce state-dependent dynamics from interaction evidence alone, without rule descriptions, reward signals, or trustworthy lexical priors. We introduce Alice, a closed-loop system that treats failed candidate updates as structural signal: when a candidate explains a new transition but loses previously explained ones, the preservation conflict reveals dynamics that the current program had conflated. Alice refines these conflicts into hypothesis classes that both provide compact, class-stratified preservation counterexamples for update and guide frontier exploration toward transitions that are novel and underrepresented with respect to the current program. We evaluate Alice on Baba in Wonderland, a prior-misaligned variant of Baba Is You that preserves simulator dynamics while replacing semantically meaningful rule-property labels with unrelated words. Experiments show that Alice substantially improves executable world-model learning under prior misalignment, and ablations show that both class refinement and class-aware exploration contribute.

</details>


### [65] [State Contamination in Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16746)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Yuen Chen,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型智能体中的"记忆洗白"现象：有毒或对抗性上下文被压缩为记忆摘要后，可逃避常规毒性检测器，但仍能影响后续生成。作者提出子阈值传播差距（SPG）量化该隐藏影响，并发现中毒状态在摘要化前进行净化可有效降低隐藏传播，而只清理已完成摘要无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖持久状态（对话记录、摘要、检索上下文、记忆缓冲区）支持长程交互，安全风险不仅来自单次输出，还来自智能体存储和重用的内容。需要研究有毒上下文被压缩压缩后逃避检测但仍影响后续行为的"记忆洗白"现象。

Method: 使用配对反事实多智能体 rollout 方法，对比有毒来源记忆摘要与中性基线对下游输出的影响；提出子阈值传播差距（Sub-threshold Propagation Gap, SPG）指标，量化在监控系统判定为安全的记忆状态下下游行为的差异。

Result: 有毒来源的记忆摘要可低于常见毒性阈值，但相比匹配的中性基线仍增加下游毒性。原始对话记录复用直接导致显式下游毒性，而压缩记忆携带隐藏的子阈值影响。在摘要化之前净化中毒状态显著减少隐藏传播差距，仅清理已完成摘要则无效。

Conclusion: 记忆增强智能体的安全性应被视作一个关于演化上下文的"状态控制问题"，净化应应用于不安全信息被压缩进持久记忆之前。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent state, including transcripts, summaries, retrieved context, and memory buffers, to support long-horizon interaction. This makes safety depend not only on individual model outputs, but also on what an agent stores and later reuses. We study a failure mode we call memory laundering: toxic or adversarial context can be compressed into memory summaries that no longer appear toxic under standard detectors, while still preserving hostile framing or conflict structure that influences future generations. Using paired counterfactual multi-agent rollouts, we show that toxic-origin memory summaries can remain below common toxicity thresholds while nevertheless increasing downstream toxicity relative to matched neutral baselines. To measure this hidden influence, we introduce the sub-threshold propagation gap (SPG), which quantifies downstream behavioral differences conditioned on memory states that a deployed monitor would classify as safe. Our experiments show that toxicity propagates through distinct state channels: raw transcript reuse drives overt downstream toxicity, while compressed memory carries hidden sub-threshold influence. We further find that mitigation depends critically on intervention placement. Sanitizing toxic state before summarization substantially reduces the hidden propagation gap, whereas cleaning only the completed summary can leave laundered influence intact. These results suggest that safety in memory-augmented agents should be treated as a state-control problem over evolving context, with sanitization applied before unsafe information is compressed into persistent memory.

</details>


### [66] [Multi-Paradigm Agent Interaction in Practice:A Systematic Analysis of Generator-Evaluator, ReAct Loop,and Adversarial Evaluation in the buddyMe Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16821)
*Xiaohua Wang,Chao Han,Kai Yu,XiaoLiang Xu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了 buddyMe 框架中三种主要智能体交互范式（多智能体编排、ReAct 工具使用循环、记忆增强交互），通过五阶段处理流水线和六维评估体系，基于四个真实案例得出关键结论：生成器-评估器预审可检测20%复杂任务的需求遗漏，ReAct 循环存在约30%冗余工具调用，对抗性评估讨论在70%场景中2-3轮达成共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体产生了多种交互范式，但很少有生产系统能够在统一架构中集成多种范式，缺乏系统性对比分析。

Method: 在 buddyMe 框架中实现三种范式，提出五阶段流水线（需求预审→任务分解→ReAct 执行→真实执行验证→对抗评估讨论）和六维加权评分评估体系，通过四个真实部署案例进行实证研究。

Result: 1) 生成器-评估器预审可检测20%复杂任务的需求遗漏，80%任务通过初审；2) ReAct 循环确保稳定子任务执行但带来约30%冗余工具调用；3) 对抗性评估讨论在近70%场景中2-3轮达成共识，主要功能为内容精炼而非逻辑反转。

Conclusion: 提供了构建稳定可靠的多范式智能体系统的实用设计指南，并与 CrewAI、AutoGen 等主流框架进行了跨范式对比。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Model (LLM) agents has produced diverse interaction paradigms, yet few production systems integrate multiple paradigms within a unified architecture. This paper presents a systematic analysis of three principal agent interaction paradigms, including Multi-Agent Orchestration (Generator-Evaluator), ReAct Tool-Use Loops, and Memory-Augmented Interaction, as implemented in buddyMe, an open-source multi-model agent programming framework. We formalize a five-stage processing pipeline: Requirement Pre-Review -> Task Decomposition -> ReAct Execution -> Real-Execution Verification -> Adversarial Evaluation Discussion, and establish a six-dimensional evaluation schema with weighted scoring. Through four empirical case studies drawn from real-world deployment logs covering museum guide generation, scheduled weather tasks, and comprehensive tour planning, we draw three key conclusions. First, Generator-Evaluator pre-review detects requirement omissions in 20 percent of complex tasks, with 80 percent tasks passing initial inspection. Second, the ReAct loop ensures stable subtask execution but leads to around 30 percent redundant tool invocations. Third, adversarial Evaluator-Defender discussions reach consensus within 2-3 rounds for nearly 70 percent of scenarios, functioning mainly for content refinement rather than logical reversal. We additionally provide three Mermaid-based architectural diagrams and conduct cross-paradigm comparisons with CrewAI, AutoGen, LangGraph, MemGPT and A-Mem across six system dimensions. The research outcomes offer practical design guidelines for constructing stable and reliable multi-paradigm agent systems.

</details>


### [67] [Voices in the Loop: Mapping Participatory AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16827)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个参与式AI倡议的开放存储库和交互式地图集，通过可重复的协议记录、整理和发布相关案例，揭示了参与式AI在地理分布、参与层级、生命周期节点等方面的模式，并展示了一个可更新、可争议、可复用的参与式默认AI基础设施的设计与治理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与式AI方法在公共、公民和人道主义场景中日益增多，但关于参与如何组织的证据仍较为零散，缺乏系统化的整理和比较框架。

Method: 基于Magaña和Shilton的Trustworthy AI语料库及额外审计案例，构建开放存储库与交互式地图集；制定了可重复的协议，涵盖发现、审核、协调、地理编码、来源追踪和发布；通过语料库层面的统计分析揭示参与式AI的地理分布、参与层级、生命周期位置、组织形式等模式。

Result: 已记录的参与式AI倡议集中在少数国家；参与最常发生在问题制定、评估和治理环节，而非模型开发或训练阶段；地图集通过版本化发布、记录关联的问题与注释通道、模式反馈工作流以及编辑/限制披露请求，实现了参与式默认AI基础设施的设计与治理框架。

Conclusion: 该地图集作为动态清单，可支持比较研究、政策学习和社区审查，具备可更新、可争议和可复用的特性。

Abstract: Participatory approaches to artificial intelligence are increasingly documented across public, civic, and humanitarian settings, but evidence about how participation is organized remains fragmented. This paper reports on the construction of an open repository and interactive atlas of participatory AI initiatives, using records harmonized from Maga~na and Shilton's Trustworthy AI corpus, and additional audited cases from research and practice. We contribute three elements. First, we specify a reproducible protocol for discovery, vetting, harmonization, geocoding, provenance tracking, and release-based publication of participatory AI records. Second, we report corpus-level patterns in geography, participation tiers, lifecycle loci, organizational form, verification status, and remaining documentation gaps. Documented initiatives remain concentrated in a small number of countries, while participation is most often coded at problem formulation, evaluation, and governance rather than model development or training. Third, we show how the atlas operationalizes a design and governance framework for participatory-by-default AI infrastructures through versioned releases, record-linked issue and annotation channels, schema feedback workflows, and redaction or restricted-disclosure requests. The atlas is intended to support comparative research, policy learning, and community scrutiny through a living inventory that can be updated, contested, and reused.

</details>


### [68] [Artificial Adaptive Intelligence: The Missing Stage Between Narrow and General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16844)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并定义了“人工自适应智能（AAI）”概念，指代无需人工指定超参数、能在多任务上保持竞争力的系统，并通过自适应指数、参数最小化原理及三条实现路径（数据/任务感知配置、结构/进化变形、训练中自适应）建立了量化框架，认为AAI是从狭义人工智能通向通用人工智能的必经阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有术语体系中，狭义人工智能（ANI）与通用人工智能（AGI）之间存在未被命名的广阔中间地带，而元学习、神经架构搜索、AutoML、持续学习、进化计算和物理信息建模等方向已悄然收敛于同一原理——将人类从参数指定回路中逐步移除。这一空白亟需一个统一术语和分析框架来描述、量化和引导该领域的发展。

Method: 本文提出了可量化的自适应指数（adaptivity index），沿与规模正交的轴衡量进度，结合系统吸收的超参数比例与针对任务专用基线的性能比率；建立了参数最小化原则（parametric minimality），将其奠基在最小描述长度（MDL）框架上，使超参数数量由数据而非设计者决定；将现有方法组织为三条路径：数据/任务感知配置、结构/进化变形、训练中自适应。

Result: 通过航空航天设计、金融制度检测、湍流建模、生态动力学和视觉-语言系统等案例研究，展示了AAI的稳定性、收敛性与治理含义。论证了ANI通往AGI的路径必然经过AAI，命名这一阶段将改变衡量标准、构建目标和对成功的定义。

Conclusion: 人类从参数指定回路中逐步退出是人工智能发展的核心趋势，AAI作为中间阶段具有理论奠基和实践指导意义，对衡量标准、构建目标和成功定义的变革将是深远的。

Abstract: Between the narrow systems we deploy and the general intelligence we speculate about lies an entire regime of machine behavior that has never received its own name. This monograph argues that this regime is not empty: it is where meta-learning, neural architecture search, AutoML, continual learning, evolutionary computation, and physics-informed modeling have quietly converged on a common principle, namely the steady removal of the human from the loop of parameter specification. We name this regime Artificial Adaptive Intelligence (AAI) and define it operationally: a system exhibits AAI to the extent that it requires no human-specified tunable hyperparameters while maintaining competitive performance across a diverse distribution of tasks. To make the definition quantitative, we introduce an adaptivity index that measures progress along an axis orthogonal to scale, combining the fraction of hyperparameters absorbed by the system with the performance ratio against a task-specialized baseline. We develop the principle of parametric minimality and ground it in the minimum description length framework, showing that the appropriate hyperparameter count is data-determined rather than designer-determined. We then organize the field around three pathways to minimality: data- and task-aware configuration, structural and evolutionary morphing, and in-training self-adaptation. We analyze their stability, convergence, and governance implications, and illustrate them through case studies spanning aerospace design, financial regime detection, turbulence modeling, ecological dynamics, and vision-language systems. The thesis is that the path from ANI to AGI passes through AAI, and that naming this stage changes what we measure, what we build, and what we call a success.

</details>


### [69] [Learning to Learn from Multimodal Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16857)
*Xingyu Sui,Weixiang Zhao,Yongxin Tang,Yanyan Zhao,Yang Wu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从多模态经验中学习的新范式，将记忆设计从预定义组件转变为自适应可学习过程，使智能体能够根据任务需求和交互历史动态构建、组织和利用记忆，实验表明该方法显著提升了多模态任务的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验驱动学习方法主要在文本环境中开发，依赖手动设计的记忆模式，难以适用于多模态环境。在多模态场景下，经验涉及感知、推理和行动等异构信号，最优记忆结构高度依赖任务且随时间演化，固定记忆设计不足以应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出从多模态经验中学习（Learning to Learn from Multimodal Experience）的新范式，将记忆设计从预定义组件转变为自适应、可学习的过程。智能体根据任务需求和交互历史动态构建、组织并利用记忆，学习如何结构化经验以提升性能。

Result: 实验表明，自适应记忆设计显著提升了智能体在多模态任务上的性能和泛化能力，验证了学习记忆机制在经验驱动学习中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 将记忆设计转化为自适应学习过程是多模态经验驱动学习的有效途径，学习记忆机制对于提升智能体在多模态环境中的表现至关重要。

Abstract: Experience-driven learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling agents to improve from interaction trajectories by accumulating and reusing past experience. However, existing approaches are predominantly developed in textual settings and rely on manually designed memory schemas, limiting their applicability to multimodal environments. In real-world scenarios, experience is inherently multimodal, involving heterogeneous signals across perception, reasoning, and action, which makes effective memory design significantly more challenging. In particular, the optimal way to structure and utilize multimodal experience is highly task-dependent and evolves over time, rendering fixed memory designs insufficient. In this work, we propose a new paradigm, learning to learn from multimodal experience, which shifts memory design from a predefined component to an adaptive and learnable process. Our framework enables agents to dynamically construct, organize, and utilize memory based on task requirements and interaction history, effectively learning how to structure experience for improved performance. Experiments demonstrate that adaptive memory design substantially enhances agent performance and generalization across multimodal tasks, highlighting the critical role of learning memory mechanisms in experience-driven learning.

</details>


### [70] [Reasoning Can Be Restored by Correcting a Few Decision Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16874)
*Changshuo Shen,Leheng Sheng,Yuxin Chen,An Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过量化基础模型与强推理模型间的token级分布差异，发现推理优势高度稀疏且集中于早期规划决策token（仅~8%的token贡献显著分歧）。据此提出分歧引导的token干预方法，在关键位置让推理模型接管一个token后立即切回基础模型，以极小代价恢复甚至超越同规模推理模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解基础模型在逐token生成过程中究竟在哪些环节出错，以及如何高效地缩小基础模型与强推理模型之间的性能差距。

Method: 通过基于似然的散度量化基础模型与推理模型间的token级分布差异，识别分歧位置；提出分歧引导的token干预（Disagreement-Guided Token Intervention），在高度分歧位置由推理模型执行单token接管，其余位置保持基础模型生成。

Result: 推理优势高度稀疏：仅约8%的token贡献显著分歧；这些token集中于响应早期、与规划决策强相关（富集17倍）、且对应基础模型的高不确定性。所提干预方法以极小干预预算即可恢复甚至超越同规模推理模型的推理性能。

Conclusion: 基础模型的主要失败点在于早期的规划决策token，这些关键token决定了后续推理轨迹的方向；通过稀疏的针对性干预可高效弥合基础模型与推理模型间的差距。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) substantially outperform their base LLM counterparts on challenging reasoning benchmarks, yet it remains poorly understood where base models go wrong during token-by-token generation and how to narrow this gap efficiently. We study the base-reasoning gap through quantifying token-level distributional disagreement between a base model and a stronger reasoning model using likelihood-based divergences. Across benchmarks, we find that the reasoning advantage is highly sparse and concentrates on a small set of early, planning-related decision tokens. For instance, on Qwen3-0.6B, only ~8% of generated tokens account for the salient disagreement, and these tokens concentrate early in the response, are strongly enriched in planning-related decisions (17x), and coincide with high base-model uncertainty -- suggesting that base models fail mainly at early planning points that steer the subsequent reasoning trajectory. Building on these findings, we propose disagreement-guided token intervention, a simple inference-time delegation scheme that performs a one-token takeover by the reasoning model only at high-disagreement positions and immediately switches back to the base model. With a small intervention budget, this sparse delegation substantially recovers and can even surpass the performance of a same-size reasoning model on challenging reasoning tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/AlphaLab-USTC/RRTokenIntervention.

</details>


### [71] [NGM: A Plug-and-Play Training-Free Memory Module for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16893)
*Yuwen Qu,Wenhui Dong,Chenyang Si,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出N-gram Memory (NGM)，一种无需训练、即插即用的记忆模块，通过因果N-gram编码器和余弦门控注入器，在不需额外训练的情况下提升大模型在下游任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件记忆模块仍依赖学习得到的记忆嵌入，需要额外训练且灵活性受限，亟需一种无需训练、可即插即用的高效知识检索机制。

Method: NGM由两个组件构成：(1) 因果N-gram编码器，直接对预训练token嵌入取平均构建N-gram表示，无需单独训练N-gram嵌入；(2) 余弦门控注入器，使用非参数化余弦门控配合ReLU将检索到的嵌入调制到上下文表示中。

Result: 在Qwen3系列（0.6B~14B）的8个基准上平均提升0.5~1.2个点，代码生成任务（LiveCodeBench +3.0）和知识密集型任务（GPQA +3.03）提升显著，多模态基准（MMStar +1.53）亦有改进。

Conclusion: NGM作为一种无需训练、即插即用的记忆模块，能够有效提升大语言模型在多种任务上的表现，尤其在代码生成和知识密集型任务上优势明显。

Abstract: Recent studies introduce conditional memory modules that decouple knowledge storage from neural computation, enabling more direct knowledge access. Compared to MoE, which relies on dynamic computation paths, explicit lookup provides a more efficient knowledge retrieval mechanism. However, these approaches still depend on learned memory embeddings, requiring additional training and limiting flexibility. To address this, we propose N-gram Memory (NGM), a training-free, plug-and-play module composed of a Causal N-Gram Encoder and a Cosine-Gated Memory Injector. The Causal N-Gram Encoder directly averages the pretrained token embeddings of the backbone model to construct N-gram representations, thereby eliminating the need to train separate N-gram embeddings from scratch. This design requires neither an additional memory table nor a retrieval pipeline. The Cosine-Gated Memory Injector then uses a non-parametric cosine gate with ReLU to modulate the retrieved embeddings into the contextual representations. We evaluate NGM on the Qwen3 series from 0.6B to 14B across eight benchmarks. NGM improves average performance by 0.5 to 1.2 points, with particularly clear gains on code generation and knowledge-intensive tasks (e.g., +3.0 on LiveCodeBench and +3.03 on GPQA for Qwen3-14B). Moreover, NGM also improves performance in multimodal benchmarks (e.g., MMStar +1.53 on Qwen3-VL-2B).

</details>


### [72] [TOBench: A Task-Oriented Omni-Modal Benchmark for Real-World Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16909)
*Zhiqiang Liu,Wenhui Dong,Yilang Tan,Yuwen Qu,Haochen Yin,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MM-ToolBench 是一个面向全模态工具使用智能体的基准测试，包含100个可执行任务、27个MCP服务器和324个工具，采用闭环多模态验证机制。测试显示最强模型Claude Opus 4.6仅达到32.0%任务成功率，远低于人类94.0%的基准水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将工具使用、计算机操作和多模态推理孤立评估，缺乏能反映现实世界端到端全模态工具使用的评估框架。

Method: 构建了包含100个可执行任务的基准测试，涵盖客户服务和智能创作两大任务族、20个子类别，基于27个MCP服务器的324个工具。核心设计为闭环多模态验证——智能体需执行工具、检查中间产物并在输出不符合要求时自我修正。采用基于MCP的执行引擎、任务特定的接地评估器，以及半自动化的场景发现、任务实例化、评估器合成和人工审核流程。

Result: 在15个当代智能体模型上测试，Claude Opus 4.6仅达到32.0%的任务成功率，人类基准为94.0%，表明该基准极具挑战性。

Conclusion: MM-ToolBench可作为评估和推动下一代全模态工具使用智能体的实用基础，通过闭环多模态验证推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are increasingly expected to operate across realistic professional workflows, where they must interpret multimodal inputs, coordinate external tools, inspect intermediate artifacts, and revise their actions before producing a final result. Existing benchmarks, however, often evaluate tool use, computer use, and multimodal reasoning in isolation, leaving a gap between benchmark settings and end-to-end omni-modal tool use in the real world. To address this gap, we introduce MM-ToolBench, a benchmark and evaluation harness for task-oriented omni-modal tool use. MM-ToolBench contains 100 executable tasks from two macro task families, Customer Service and Intelligent Creation, covering 20 subcategory slices and supported by 27 MCP servers with 324 tools. The central design of MM-ToolBench is closed-loop multimodal verification: agents must execute tools, inspect rendered or transformed artifacts, and self-correct when outputs fail task-specific requirements. To make such evaluation scalable and verifiable, MM-ToolBench couples MCP-based execution with task-specific grounded evaluators and a semi-automated construction pipeline for scenario discovery, task instantiation, evaluator synthesis, and human audit.
