Mechanistically, attention analysis shows that L-updates are consistently more local than H-updates in both Sudoku and Maze. Together, these results show that, in a two-state recurrent setting, a clear state-identity signal can induce stable, related functional roles inside a shared-parameter recurrent Transformer. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/juchengshen/air}{\textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/juchengshen/air}}.

</details>


### [145] [Wavelet Flow Matching for Multi-Scale Physics Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16573)
*Gabriele Accarino,Juan Nathaniel,Carla Roesch,Pierre Gentine,Sara Shamekh,Duncan Watson-Parris,Viviana Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Wavelet Flow Matching (WFM)，利用小波变换在U-Net中直接执行最优传输，无需预训练自编码器，实现对偏微分方程控制的多尺度物理系统的长期稳定、高精度生成式仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性仿真器产生过度平滑预测，生成式方法虽能捕捉细节但计算成本高；潜空间生成模型需额外预训练自编码器。需要一种兼顾计算效率与生成质量的多尺度物理仿真方法。

Method: WFM将最优传输直接构建在小波空间中，利用U-Net的分层结构联合预测预设小波表示的传输速度，无需单独训练压缩模型，实现训练自由的多尺度生成式仿真。

Result: 在三个混沌流体动力学系统上，WFM在长期稳定性、预测精度和频谱一致性上全面超越现有最优模型。

Conclusion: 小波空间是一种有效的免训练表示方法，可显著提升复杂物理动力学的生成式仿真性能。

Abstract: Accurate emulation of multi-scale physical systems governed by PDEs demands models that remain stable over long autoregressive rollouts while preserving fine-scale structures. Deterministic emulators produce overly-smoothed predictions, while generative approaches better capture details but are costly. Latent-space generative models have emerged as a compromise but with the additional cost of separately pre-trained autoencoders. We propose Wavelet Flow Matching (WFM), a novel generative emulator that overcomes current trade-offs between cost and skill by performing optimal-transport directly in the multi-scale wavelet space. Rather than learning a latent compression, WFM leverages the hierarchical structure of a U-Net to jointly predict transport velocities of a prescribed wavelet representation. On three challenging systems of chaotic fluid dynamics, WFM achieves superior long-horizon stability, accuracy and spectral coherence compared to state-of-the-art models. Our results clearly position the wavelet space as an effective training-free representation for generative emulation of complex physical dynamics.

</details>


### [146] [Structure-Aware Masking for Protein Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16581)
*Thomas Walton,Ayan Goel,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于结构感知的Bucket Masking策略，通过优先掩盖三维空间中结构耦合的残基区域，在蛋白质适应度预测任务上相比随机掩码提升高达14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLM训练以固定速率随机掩盖单个残基，隐含假设所有序列位置对表示学习贡献均等，但蛋白质功能依赖三维结构的长程相互作用，随机掩盖无法有效捕捉这些非局部耦合关系。

Method: 根据残基在三维空间中的邻近性（即残基接触信息）构建分组，训练过程中优先掩盖结构耦合区域，从而将学习目标导向建模长程相互作用。

Result: 在四个蛋白质适应度预测任务上，Bucket Masking相比标准随机掩码提升最高达14%，尤其在高阶突变相互作用预测上表现优异。消融实验证实改进源于掩码位置（而非跨度大小），表明掩码可作为一种位置归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 结构感知的掩码策略能有效引入蛋白质三维结构信息作为归纳偏置，提升蛋白质语言模型在功能预测任务上的表现。

Abstract: Masked language modeling (MLM) is the standard objective for training protein language models, typically implemented by randomly masking individual residues at a fixed rate (e.g., 15%). This practice implicitly assumes that all sequence positions contribute equally to representation learning. In downstream fitness prediction tasks, however, protein sequences are governed by three-dimensional structural dependencies and long-range residue contacts that induce strong nonlocal couplings between residues. We introduce Bucket Masking, a structure-aware masking strategy that selects groups of residues based on their proximity in three-dimensional space, preferentially masking structurally coupled regions during training. By conditioning the masking distribution on residue contacts, Bucket Masking shifts the learning objective toward modeling long-range interactions that are critical for protein function. Across four downstream protein fitness prediction tasks, Bucket Masking enables up to a 14% improvement over standard random masking, excelling at predicting higher-order mutational interactions. Through controlled ablations, we show that these improvements arise from mask placement rather than span size, establishing masking as a positional inductive bias.

</details>


### [147] [Proximal basin hopping: global optimization with guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18364)
*Guillaume Lauga,Cesare Molinari,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Proximal Basin Hopping (PBH)的新理论框架，结合近端优化和局部最小化，构建了一个能以高概率收敛到全局最小值的实用算法，在高维问题上表现尤为出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局优化问题充满挑战，现有算法虽在经验上成功但缺乏理论支撑，亟需一个既有理论保证又有实际效用的优化框架。

Method: 提出Proximal Basin Hopping (PBH)理论框架，精心融合了近端优化（proximal optimization）与局部最小化（local minimization）策略，并基于此构建了实用算法。

Result: 该算法在有限样本下以高概率收敛到全局最优解；在标准合成硬函数和实际问题（如深度学习标度律拟合）上均优于有理论保证的知名算法；且维度越高，性能差距越大。

Conclusion: PBH框架成功弥合了全局优化中理论与实践之间的鸿沟，尤其在高维场景下展现出显著优势，为全局优化提供了新的理论视角和高效工具。

Abstract: Global optimization is a challenging problem, with plenty of algorithms displaying empirical success, but scarce theoretical backing. In this work, we propose a new theoretical framework called Proximal Basin Hopping (PBH), carefully tailored to combine proximal optimization and local minimization. We use it to construct a practical algorithm that converges to the global minimizer with high probability, when using a finite amount of samples. Proximal Basin Hopping outperforms well known algorithms with theoretical backing on standard synthetic hard functions, and real problems such as fitting scaling laws for deep learning. Furthermore, the higher the dimension, the better the performance gap.

</details>


### [148] [How Few-Shot Examples Add Up: A Causal Decomposition of Function Vectors in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16591)
*Entang Wang,Yiwei Wang,Aleksandra Bakalova,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示少样本提示中的函数向量（FV）可分解为示例级子FV的线性组合，且模型会根据上下文自适应地重新加权各示例的贡献，其中Query-Key对齐在模糊情境下对FV质量起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏对少样本提示如何通过函数向量（FV）驱动任务行为的机制性解释，尤其是示例间如何相互作用以及上下文如何影响FV的构成。

Method: 通过线性分解验证n-shot FV可近似为示例级子FV的线性组合；分析注意力机制如何根据上下文重新加权示例贡献；使用因果分解区分Query-Key路由与Value更新的作用。

Result: FV具有加性和可组合性；模型会根据信息量和模糊性自适应地重新加权示例；Query-Key对齐在模糊情境下对FV质量贡献最稳定，而Value介导的效果更具异质性。

Conclusion: 该研究将加性叠加与上下文依赖的注意力重新加权统一为一种可检验的机制性解释，阐明了少样本提示如何实现任务执行。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) excels at new tasks from minimal examples, yet we still lack a mechanistic explanation of how few-shot prompts shape a model's function vector (FV)--a causal activation direction that drives task behavior on the ICL query. Across tasks and models, an $n$-shot FV is well-approximated by a linear combination of example-level sub-FVs, suggesting additive and composable contributions from individual demonstrations. Beyond additivity, we show that models contextualize individual examples' representations based on prior examples to adaptively reweight which demonstrations dominate the FV: attention shifts toward examples that are more informative and less ambiguous under the context. Finally, a causal decomposition separates Query-Key routing from Value updates, finding that contextualization's most consistent contributions to FV quality arise from Query-Key alignment--particularly in ambiguous settings--while Value-mediated effects are more heterogeneous. Together, these results unify additive superposition with context-dependent attention reweighting into a mechanistic, testable account of how few-shot prompts implement tasks.

</details>


### [149] [Spherical Harmonic Optimal Transport: Application to Climate Models Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18389)
*Pierre Houédry,Iskander Legheraba,Léo Buecher,Nicolas Courty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明热核代价在时间趋零时收敛到最优传输代价（平衡与不平衡情形），在二维球面上保证Sinkhorn散度保留经典OT的几何分析性质，并利用球谐结构提出O(n)内存、O(n^{3/2})每迭代时间的快速Sinkhorn算法，在气候模型评估中验证了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输计算成本高昂，阻碍其实际应用；流形上的热核卷积算法理论性质尚不清晰，需要建立其理论保证并设计高效算法。

Method: 理论分析热核代价的收敛性（平衡与不平衡情形）；在S^2上利用球谐函数分解推导快速Sinkhorn算法，实现O(n)内存和O(n^{3/2})每步迭代时间，运算完全密集且GPU友好。

Result: 热核代价在时间趋零时收敛到最优传输代价；在S^2上Sinkhorn散度保留经典OT的几何与分析性质；算法在合成数据上验证了计算效率。

Conclusion: 为流形上的最优传输提供了理论保证和高效算法，并在全球气候模型评估中展示了空间和季节维度的应用潜力。

Abstract: Optimal transport provides a powerful framework for comparing measures while respecting the geometry of their support, but comes with an expensive computational cost, hindering its potential application to real world use cases. On manifolds, convolutional algorithms based on the heat kernel have been proposed to alleviate this cost, but their theoretical properties remain largely unexplored. We establish that the heat kernel cost converges to the optimal transport cost as time vanishes in the balanced and unbalanced cases. In the specific case of the 2-sphere $\mathbb{S}^2$, we ensure that the associated Sinkhorn divergences retains the desirable geometric and analytic properties of classical optimal transport discrepancies. Moreover, we leverage the harmonic structure of the sphere to derive a fast Sinkhorn algorithm, requiring only $\mathcal{O}(n)$ memory and $\mathcal{O}(n^{3/2})$ time per iteration, with fully dense GPU-friendly operations. We validate its computational efficiency on synthetic data, and discuss its potential use in the evaluation of global climate models, providing both spatial and seasonal insights into models performances.

</details>


### [150] [Where Pretraining writes and Alignment reads: the asymmetry of Transformer weight space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16600)
*Valeria Ruscio,Eli-Shaoul Khedouri,Keiran Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过相对子空间分数探针，揭示了交叉熵预训练与偏好对齐在Transformer权重中留下的几何不同痕迹：对齐更新集中在读取路径（W_Q, W_K）沿注意力输入激活的主方向，而在写入路径（W_O, W_2）中相对于预测子空间保持近各向同性。作者通过各向异性梯度累积机制解释了这一模式，并提供了因果证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解交叉熵预训练与偏好对齐两种训练范式在Transformer权重空间中留下的不同几何痕迹，揭示其不对称性背后的机制。

Method: 提出相对子空间分数探针，追踪权重增量与残差流激活子空间及解嵌入预测子空间的对齐程度；通过各向异性梯度累积理论（外积累加导致方向结构继承自协方差集中的一侧）解释观察到的模式；利用checkpoint内轨迹、对比目标控制和封闭形式秩1干预进行因果验证。

Result: 偏好对齐的权重增量集中在读取路径（W_Q, W_K）沿注意力输入激活的主方向，在写入路径（W_O, W_2）中相对于预测子空间保持近各向同性。交叉熵预训练在写入路径产生预测几何结构，而对齐目标几乎不增加额外的写入侧浓度。

Conclusion: 交叉熵预训练和偏好对齐在权重空间中留下几何不对称的痕迹，这种不对称性源于梯度各向异性的差异：读取路径由输入激活的尖峰协方差主导（目标无关），写入路径由上游梯度的各向异性主导（依赖于损失函数）。

Abstract: Cross-entropy pretraining and preference alignment update the same transformer weights, but leave geometrically distinct traces. We characterise this asymmetry with a relative-subspace-fraction probe that tracks how weight deltas align with residual-stream activation subspaces and with the prediction subspace defined by the unembedding. Alignment deltas concentrate in the read pathway ($W_Q$, $W_K$), along principal directions of attention-input activations, while remaining near-isotropic in the write pathway ($W_O$, $W_2$) relative to the prediction subspace. We explain this pattern through anisotropic gradient accumulation: updates to a matrix $W$ are sums of outer products $δ_t a_t^\top$, and inherit directional structure from whichever side has concentrated covariance. For read-pathway matrices, this side is the input activation $a_t$, whose covariance is spiked in trained transformers and therefore produces objective-agnostic concentration. For write-pathway matrices, the relevant side is the upstream gradient $δ_t$, whose anisotropy depends on the loss. Cross-entropy supplies the canonical sharp per-sample signal, inducing write-pathway prediction geometry during pretraining; alignment objectives typically add little further write-side concentration. We support this explanation with a within-checkpoint trajectory, a graded contrastive-objective control, and a closed-form rank-1 intervention with matched direction controls, providing causal evidence for the proposed weight-space geometry.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [A Projected Stochastic Gradient Method for Finite-Sum Problems with Linear Equality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16263)
*Natasa Krklec Jerinkic,Benedetta Morini,Mahsa Yousefi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种求解线性约束下有限和最小化问题的随机梯度方法，该方法将投影梯度法扩展到使用随机梯度和可能不精确的投影映射，并在标准假设下给出了与无约束问题理论一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度方法多关注无约束问题，而实际应用中常涉及线性约束。本文旨在将随机梯度法推广到确定性线性约束场景，同时允许使用不精确投影以提升实用性。

Method: 在凸集上的投影梯度法框架中，引入随机梯度替代全梯度，并允许投影映射存在不精确性。在随机梯度方法的标准假设下进行理论分析。

Result: 理论结果与无约束随机梯度法的经典结论一致；数值实验展示了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在理论和实践上均有效，为线性约束下的随机优化提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: A stochastic gradient method for finite-sum minimization subject to deterministic linear constraints is proposed and analyzed. The procedure presented adapts the projected gradient method on convex set to the use of both a stochastic gradient and a possibly inexact projection map. Under standard assumptions in the field of stochastic gradient methods, we provide theoretical results in agreement with the theory for unconstrained problems. Numerical results are presented to show the practical behavior of the procedure.

</details>


### [152] [Static Output Feedback Stabilization of Linear Systems with Multiple Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16631)
*Danilo Braghini,Eduardo S. Tognetti,Matthew M. Peet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于静态输出反馈(SOF)的时滞系统镇定新方法，通过将面向常微分方程(ODE)的凸优化方法扩展至时滞系统，利用投影引理从矩阵到部分积分(PI)算子的推广，显著降低了保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对时滞系统的静态输出反馈控制方法存在保守性高的问题，需要一种能有效降低保守性的新方法。

Method: 1) 提出一种新的状态空间表示将SOF凸优化方法从ODE扩展到时滞系统；2) 将线性矩阵不等式(LMI)扩展至无限维系统，构建两个凸优化问题；3) 将投影引理从矩阵推广到部分积分(PI)算子，用于第二个优化问题。

Result: 与现有文献中其他SOF方法相比，该方法在时滞系统镇定方面显著降低了保守性。

Conclusion: 所提出的SOF控制方法是有效的，能够以更低的保守性实现时滞系统的镇定。

Abstract: This work proposes a new procedure for the stabilization of time-delay systems using Static Output Feedback (SOF) control. A previous convex optimization approach to SOF for Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) is extended to time-delay systems through the use of a proposed state-space representation. This approach is based on solving two convex optimization problems, which are extensions of Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs) to infinite-dimensional systems. The first problem is stabilization under state feedback control; the second problem takes advantage of the Projection Lemma, which is extended here from matrices to Partial Integral (PI) operators. Finally, the results are compared with other SOF solutions for systems with delay found in the literature, showing a significant reduction in conservatism.

</details>


### [153] [Black-Box Followers, White-Box Leaders: Partial Zeroth-Order Methods for MPECs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16685)
*Miriam Fischer,Dario Paccagnan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出PZOS算法，在求解数学规划与均衡约束问题时，将零阶方法仅用于未知的非光滑追随者响应，而对领导者的已知成本函数使用精确梯度，从而在方差和收敛速度上优于黑箱基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在领导者知道自身成本函数但缺乏追随者响应模型的情况下，现有零阶方法将整个组合目标视为黑箱，浪费了领导者已知成本函数的信息，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出PZOS算法，将领导者成本的精确偏导数与追随者响应的零阶雅可比估计以链式法则方式结合；引入部分Goldstein次微分作为针对该组合结构的驻点概念。

Result: PZOS的方差界严格低于黑箱基线；算法收敛到标准驻点和部分Goldstein驻点；在交通博弈的通行费优化和安全博弈的攻防投资两个应用中，收敛速度、目标值和估计方差均优于黑箱基线，甚至在每轮查询次数较少时也表现稳健。

Conclusion: 通过将零阶方法仅应用于真正未知的部分，可以显著提升数学规划与均衡约束问题的求解效率，为结合已知结构信息与无模型优化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: We study mathematical programs with equilibrium constraints, in which a leader knows their own cost function, but lacks a model of the followers' response. Instead, the leader can only query this response at specific points. While this setting precludes the use of gradient-based methods, existing zeroth-order approaches treat the composed objective entirely as a black box, deploying zeroth-order tools across both the leader and follower. Such approaches are inefficient, as they discard information the leader already possesses about their own cost function. In this work we instead propose to deploy zeroth-order tools only where they are truly needed: to handle the unknown, non-smooth followers' response. Specifically, we first propose PZOS, an algorithm that combines exact partial gradients of the leader's cost with zeroth-order Jacobian estimates of the followers' response in a chain-rule-inspired manner, and establish that it achieves a strictly lower variance bound than the black-box baseline. Second, we introduce the partial Goldstein subdifferential, a stationarity notion tailored to this composite structure, and prove convergence of our algorithm to both standard and partial Goldstein stationary points. Finally, we validate our method on two application domains -- toll optimization in routing games and defense-attack investment in security games -- demonstrating consistent improvements over black-box baselines in convergence speed, objective value, and estimator variance, with robust performance even under few queries per iteration.

</details>


### [154] [New Bounds for Exact Penalized Cardinality-Constrained Optimization with Pseudonormality Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16687)
*Lili Pan,Huilin Xie,Xianchao Xiu,Jiyuan Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带基数约束的优化问题，将伪正规性条件扩展到基数约束框架，建立了不含Lipschitz连续性假设的局部精确罚函数理论，并分析了投影次梯度方法及其随机变体的收敛性，给出了比现有结果更精确的迭代序列和函数值边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基数约束优化在稀疏学习和信号恢复中应用广泛，但由于基数约束的非凸性和不连续性，求解十分困难。现有精确罚理论通常需要Lipschitz连续性假设，本文旨在放松这一条件，建立更一般的精确罚理论。

Method: 将伪正规性条件(pseudonormality condition)推广到带有一般等式和不等式约束的基数约束优化框架中，在不假设目标函数Lipschitz连续的情况下证明局部精确罚函数性质；基于导出精确罚形式，分析投影次梯度方法及其随机变体的收敛性。

Result: 建立了基数约束优化下无需Lipschitz连续性的局部精确罚理论；给出了投影次梯度方法及其随机变体更精确的迭代序列界和目标函数值界。

Conclusion: 本文为基数约束优化提供了更一般的精确罚理论框架，并且推导的收敛性边界比现有结果更精确，对稀疏学习和信号恢复等领域具有理论意义。

Abstract: Cardinality-constrained optimization (CCO) is a popular topic in sparse learning and signal recovery, yet remains challenging due to the inherent nonconvexity and discontinuity of cardinality constraints. This paper investigates the exact penalty theory for CCO problems with general equality and inequality constraints. In particular, we extend the pseudonormality condition to the cardinality-constrained framework and establish the local exact penalization without imposing Lipschitz continuity on the objective function. We further analyze both the projected subgradient method and its stochastic variant with convergence guarantees for the derived exact penalty formulation. Compared with the existing results, we give some more precise bounds of the iterate sequence and the objective function value.

</details>


### [155] [A Proximal Gradient Framework for Composite Multiobjective Optimization on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16731)
*Kangming Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黎曼多目标邻近梯度方法（RMPGM），用于流形上的复合优化问题，直接处理向量值目标，建立全局收敛到Pareto驻点及O(1/k)收敛率，并开发了非精确和信赖域两种变体，数值实验表明优于次梯度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形上的多目标优化方法多基于标量化技术，缺乏直接处理向量值目标且具有理论保证的梯度类方法。

Method: 提出黎曼多目标邻近梯度法（RMPGM），直接处理向量值目标；进一步开发非精确变体（允许子问题受控非精确求解）和信赖域变体（自适应调整罚参数）。

Result: RMPGM全局收敛到Pareto驻点，收敛率为O(1/k)；信赖域变体达到O(ε^{-2})迭代复杂度；数值实验一致优于次梯度基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的RMPGM框架及其变体为流形上的复合多目标优化提供了一种有效且具有理论保证的求解方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Riemannian Multiobjective Proximal Gradient Method (RMPGM) for composite optimization problems on manifolds. Unlike scalarization-based approaches, the proposed framework directly handles vector-valued objectives and establishes global convergence to Pareto stationary points, together with an $\mathcal{O}(1/k)$ convergence rate. We further develop two variants to enhance practicality and performance: an inexact RMPGM that allows controlled inexactness in solving subproblems, and a trust-region RMPGM that adaptively adjusts the penalty parameter and achieves an $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2}) $iteration complexity. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods are consistently outperform subgradient-based baselines.

</details>


### [156] [Data-Driven Linear Quadratic Control Using Output-Feedback via Non-Minimal Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16752)
*Weijian Li,Bowen Yi,Panos J. Antsaklis,Hai Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对系统矩阵未知、仅可获取输入输出数据的连续时间线性二次型控制问题，提出了一种基于Kreisselmeier自适应滤波器的输出反馈学习框架，通过非最小实现构造增广系统，并开发了数据驱动值迭代算法，仿真验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决系统矩阵未知情况下，仅利用输入输出数据实现连续时间线性二次型最优控制的问题，弥补现有方法对状态信息或系统模型依赖的局限性。

Method: 基于Kreisselmeier自适应滤波器构建规范非最小实现，利用其观测器解释构造增广系统，保持输入输出响应并提供可访问的状态轨迹；证明增广系统最优增益可恢复原始系统的最优状态反馈解，并据此在自适应动态规划框架下开发数据驱动值迭代算法。

Result: 所提出的控制器仅依靠输入输出数据即可实现，并通过仿真验证了其性能有效性。

Conclusion: 该输出反馈学习框架成功实现了在系统矩阵未知条件下，仅利用输入输出数据的连续时间LQR最优控制，为实际工程中模型不可知场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a continuous-time linear quadratic control problem for systems with unknown matrices, where only input-output data are available. We propose an output-feedback learning framework based on a canonical nonminimal realization constructed through Kreisselmeier's adaptive filter. The filter admits an observer interpretation, which leads to an augmented system that preserves the input-output response of the realization and provides accessible state trajectories. We show that the optimal gain of this augmented system explicitly recovers the optimal gain associated with the canonical non-minimal realization, and hence achieves the optimal state-feedback solution of the original plant. Exploiting this relation and the known structure of the augmented input matrix, we develop a data-driven value iteration algorithm within the adaptive dynamic programming framework. The resulting controller is implementable from input-output data, and its performance is validated via simulations.

</details>


### [157] [A Diagnostic Framework for Implementation Risk in Bilevel Decision Problems: The Ambiguity Premium and the Robustness--Efficiency Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16780)
*Jiguang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，通过计算领导者决策在ε-最优跟随者响应集上的乐观与悲观上层值之差（即模糊性溢价），量化双层规划中因跟随者响应非唯一或仅近最优可验证而导致的上层结果不确定性，并建立直径界和√ε阶估计，结合GNEP重构提出筛选工作流，通过两个案例展示鲁棒性-效率前沿的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双层规划中，跟随者的最优响应可能不唯一，或仅能验证其近最优行为，导致同一领导者决策可能引发一系列上层结果。现有研究缺乏量化这种暴露风险的诊断工具。

Method: 定义模糊性溢价Δ_ε(x)为悲观与乐观上层值之差；建立直径界Δ_ε(x)≤L_F(x)·diam(S_ε(x))及二次下层增长下的O(√ε)估计；组织现有双层-GNEP重构的计算角色，提出包含名义值、模糊性暴露和一阶残差的筛选工作流。

Result: 给出了模糊性溢价的上下界理论估计；通过并行链路Stackelberg定价问题和含多样化约束的凸发电规划两个案例，展示了如何利用鲁棒性-效率前沿识别名义上吸引但对近最优跟随者响应敏感的候选策略。

Conclusion: 该诊断框架使经典的乐观-悲观双层区分可操作化为实施风险诊断工具，为决策者提供兼顾名义表现与鲁棒性的策略评估视角。

Abstract: Hierarchical decision problems are often modeled as bilevel programs in which a leader commits to a policy and a follower responds optimally. When the follower's optimal response is nonunique, or when only near-optimal follower behavior can be verified, the same leader decision may induce a range of upper-level outcomes. This paper develops a diagnostic framework for quantifying that exposure. For a leader decision $x$, we evaluate the optimistic and pessimistic upper-level values over the $ε$-optimal follower response set $S_ε(x)$ and use their difference, \[
  Δ_ε(x):=ψ_ε^p(x)-ψ_ε^o(x), \] as an ambiguity premium. The premium itself is classical in the optimistic--pessimistic bilevel distinction; the contribution here is to make it operational as an implementation-risk diagnostic. We establish a diameter bound $Δ_ε(x)\le L_F(x)\,\mathrm{diam}(S_ε(x))$ and an $\mathcal{O}(\sqrtε)$ estimate under quadratic lower-level growth. We then organize existing bilevel--GNEP reformulations by their computational roles and propose a screening workflow that reports, for each candidate policy, nominal value, ambiguity exposure, and a first-order residual. Two stylized case studies -- a parallel-link Stackelberg pricing problem and a convex generation-planning model with diversification constraints -- show how the resulting robustness--efficiency frontier can identify policies that are nominally attractive but sensitive to near-optimal follower responses.

</details>


### [158] [Stochastic Optimization and Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16875)
*Arutyun Avetisyan,Darina Dvinskikh,Alexander Gasnikov,Vladimir Temlyakov,Nazarii Tupitsa,Denis Turdakov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从统计学和统计学习角度出发，将随机优化问题转化为最大化对数似然或最小化总体风险，并简要描述了两种主要求解期望最小化问题的方法：离线方法（蒙特卡洛/样本平均近似）和在线方法（随机近似）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从统计和统计学习的视角出发，将随机优化问题与对数似然最大化和总体风险最小化相联系，为求解期望最小化问题提供理论基础。

Method: 分别介绍了离线方法（蒙特卡洛/样本平均近似）和在线方法（随机近似）两种处理期望最小化问题的主要途径。

Result: 明确了从统计视角看待随机优化问题的框架，并区分了离线和在线两类求解方法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的统计视角来理解随机优化问题，并概述了离线和在线两类主要求解策略。

Abstract: This paper aims to motivate stochastic optimization problems from a statistical perspective and a statistical learning perspective, where the goal is to maximize the log-likelihood or minimize the population risk. We briefly describe the two main approaches: offline (Monte Carlo / Sample Average Approximation) and online (Stochastic Approximation) approaches -- to solve the expectation minimization problems.

</details>


### [159] [Clipped Stochastic Gradient Tracking For Locally Smooth Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17027)
*Leilei Mei,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出相对一致连续性（RUC）正则条件刻画局部光滑常数的函数行为，并基于此设计了一种带交错方差缩减的裁剪梯度跟踪方法，在仅依赖目标函数局部光滑性的条件下实现了O(∑_i n_i^{1.5} + n_i^{0.5}ε^{-1})的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度跟踪方法（包括自适应方法）通常依赖全局光滑常数，但实际问题中局部光滑常数在定义域内变化剧烈甚至无界，使用全局上界过于保守；同时骤变的步长会给分布式算法的收敛性和一致性分析带来困难，直接使用局部光滑常数存在理论挑战。

Method: 提出相对一致连续性（RUC）正则条件来刻画局部光滑常数作为集合函数的增长行为，覆盖常数、对数、多项式乃至指数等常见增长函数；针对具有有限和结构的RUC正则分布式优化问题，设计了一种带交错方差缩减的裁剪梯度跟踪方法，仅依赖于目标函数的局部光滑性。

Result: 建立了O(∑_i n_i^{1.5} + n_i^{0.5}ε^{-1})的复杂度保证。

Conclusion: RUC条件有效解决了局部光滑常数在分布式算法分析中的理论难题，所提方法在更现实的局部光滑假设下实现了收敛保证。

Abstract: Most stochastic gradient tracking (GT) methods adopt pre-scheduled stepsize rules, while a few recent works studied adaptive stepsizes that attempt to respond to the problem's local landscape. These methods are typically built upon the problem's global smoothness constant in both analysis and implementation, even for the adaptive ones. On the one hand, for many problems the local smoothness constant may vary drastically across the domain, and sometimes even unbounded, using the global upper bound of the local constants is too conservative. On the other hand, drastic stepsize changes can cause difficulties in the analysis of convergence and consensus of distributed algorithms, making the direct use of local smoothness constants risky and theoretically challenging. In this paper, we propose a \emph{Relative Uniform Continuity} (RUC) regularity condition for the local smoothness constant as a function of sets. The RUC condition covers most common growth functions for local smoothness constant, ranging from constant and logarithmic to polynomial and even exponential. For RUC-regular distributed optimization problems with finite-sum structure, we derive a clipped gradient tracking method with staggered variance reduction, which only relies on the local smoothness of objective functions, and an $\mathcal{O}(\sum_in_i^{1.5}+n_i^{0.5}ε^{-1})$ complexity has been established for our algorithm.

</details>


### [160] [A preconditioned augmented Lagrangian method for solving semidefinite programming problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17089)
*Tianyun Tang,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预处理增广拉格朗日方法（ALM）求解半定规划（SDP）问题，通过加权罚函数实现预处理器，权重矩阵由可行域切空间上的投影算子导出。该方法与可行方法SDPF结合形成SDPF+求解器，能处理含非线性目标函数的凸问题。大量数值实验表明，SDPF+在低秩结构的大规模SDP问题上效率与鲁棒性优于其他求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ALM方法在求解病态SDP问题时收敛缓慢，需要一种有效的预处理技术来加速收敛，特别是针对大规模且具有低秩结构的最优解问题。

Method: 在ALM子问题中引入加权罚函数，权重矩阵由可行域切空间上的投影算子导出，作为预处理器；将此预处理ALM与已有可行方法SDPF结合，构建SDPF+求解器。

Result: 大量数值实验表明SDPF+在低秩结构的大规模SDP问题上具有更高的效率和鲁棒性，整体性能优于其他求解器。

Conclusion: 预处理ALM通过切空间投影加权罚函数显著加速了病态SDP问题的求解，SDPF+作为一种通用SDP求解器，在低秩大规模问题上具有竞争力。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a preconditioned augmented Lagrangian method (ALM) for solving semidefinite programming (SDP) problems. The preconditioner is implemented via a weighted penalty function in the ALM subproblem, with the weight matrix derived from the projection operator onto the tangent space of the feasible region. This simple yet effective modification significantly accelerates ALM, particularly for ill-conditioned SDPs. By combining the preconditioned ALM with our previously developed feasible method SDPF, we develop SDPF+, an SDP solver capable of handling convex problems with possibly nonlinear objective functions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of SDPF+, showing that it can generally outperform other solvers on large-scale SDPs whose optimal solutions exhibit low-rank structure.

</details>


### [161] [QPLEX Decision Processes: Formulation via Nonlinear Markov Chains and Optimization via Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17149)
*Antonius B. Dieker,Steven T. Hackman,Zitong Wang,Yunhao Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出QPLEX决策过程(QDP)，将QPLEX计算建模方法融入非线性马尔可夫决策框架，用于处理具有非平稳到达、一般服务分布和服务水平机会约束的排队系统动态控制，通过降维和确定性梯度计算有效克服维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排队系统的动态控制面临维数灾难，特别是当服务时间服从一般分布时状态空间急剧膨胀；同时，非平稳到达和服务水平机会约束进一步增加了问题的复杂性。

Method: 提出QPLEX决策过程(QDP)模型，将QPLEX计算建模方法集成到非线性马尔可夫决策框架中；利用QPLEX近似中非线性转移概率实现状态空间数量级缩减；采用前向和后向迭代方案在缩小状态空间上确定性计算梯度；结合块对角Fisher近似的自然梯度方法进行优化。

Result: 在单站动态定价问题中，当奖励结构使用等待和终端成本时，可在单CPU上数秒内找到近优策略；当使用服务水平机会约束偏离惩罚时，虽优化难度显著增加，仍可在约一分钟内找到高质量实用策略。

Conclusion: QDP有效解决了具有一般服务分布的排队系统动态控制中的维数灾难，能够快速生成近优策略，对实际排队系统控制具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a QPLEX Decision Process (QDP) as a model for dynamic control of queueing systems with non-stationary arrivals, general service distributions, and service-level chance constraints. QDPs integrate QPLEX, a computational modeling methodology for transient analysis of stochastic systems, into a nonlinear Markov decision framework. Since QPLEX approximations use nonlinear transition probabilities with orders-of-magnitude smaller state spaces, QDPs circumvent the curse of dimensionality associated with general service times. Via forward and backward iterative schemes, we can rapidly compute gradients deterministically on the much smaller state space, eliminating sampling variance. We further address optimization through natural-gradient-inspired methods with block-diagonal Fisher approximations. To illustrate the QDP methodology, we formulate a single-station dynamic pricing problem with non-stationary demand as a QDP. When the reward structure uses waiting and terminal costs, our approach can find near-optimal policies in seconds on a single CPU; when the reward structure uses penalties for deviating from service-level chance constraints, the optimization landscape is substantially more challenging yet our approach can find a high-quality, practical policy in approximately a minute on a single CPU.

</details>


### [162] [Scalable Bi-causal Optimal Transport via KL Relaxation and Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17271)
*Haoyang Cao,Jesse Hoekstra,Renyuan Xu,Yumin Xu,Ruixun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的随机优化框架，通过KL散度罚函数松弛双因果最优传输问题中的硬边际约束，并设计了具有无偏估计、方差缩减和非渐近遗憾保证的策略梯度算法，实现了路径空间上双因果耦合的高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双因果最优传输在鲁棒金融、序贯不确定性量化和多阶段随机优化中具有重要意义，但其在连续分布和长时域下的计算困难限制了实际应用，尤其是路径空间上双因果耦合约束的强制执行具有挑战性。

Method: 提出KL惩罚松弛方法，将硬边际约束替换为可处理的散度惩罚并保留递归结构；建立原始和松弛问题的动态规划原理；证明松弛问题随惩罚增大收敛到原始问题；推导松弛目标的显式策略梯度表示；设计基于策略梯度的实用算法，包含无偏小批量估计、方差缩减和非渐近遗憾保证。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法能准确捕捉边际分布和时间依赖关系，在鲁棒子对冲和时间序列统计降尺度等应用中表现良好。

Conclusion: 该工作为双因果OT提供了一种可扩展的计算方法，拓宽了其在非预期信息约束关键场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Bi-causal optimal transport (OT) is a natural framework for comparing and coupling stochastic processes under nonanticipative information constraints, with important applications in robust finance, sequential uncertainty quantification, and multistage stochastic optimization. In particular, a learned bi-causal coupling naturally serves as a simulator for generating joint sample paths that respect both prescribed marginal laws and the underlying information flow. Its practical use, however, is limited by the computational difficulty of enforcing bi-causal coupling constraints over path space, especially for continuous distributions and long horizons. We develop a scalable stochastic-optimization framework for computing bi-causal OT couplings under general marginals. Our approach introduces a Kullback--Leibler (KL)-penalized relaxation that replaces hard marginal constraints with tractable divergence penalties while preserving the recursive structure of the problem. We establish dynamic programming principles for both the original and relaxed formulations, prove that the relaxed problem converges to the original bi-causal OT problem as the penalty grows, and derive explicit policy-gradient representations for the relaxed objective. Building on these results, we propose a practical policy-gradient algorithm with unbiased mini-batch estimators, variance reduction, and nonasymptotic regret guarantees. Numerical experiments show that the method accurately captures marginal laws and temporal dependence, and performs well in applications including robust subhedging and time series statistical downscaling. These results provide a scalable computational approach to bi-causal OT and broaden its applicability in settings where nonanticipative information constraints are essential.

</details>


### [163] [Speeding Up Nonsmooth Bayesian MCMC Sampling via Inexact Proximal Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17306)
*Susan Ghaderi,Alireza Kabgani,Yves Moreau,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出iPULA算法，一种用于非光滑复合势能后验采样的非精确近端非调整朗之万算法，通过允许近端算子的近似计算并建立非渐近收敛保证，在医学图像重建等任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于近端的朗之万方法在理论上适用于非光滑复合势能的后验采样，但实践中限于具有闭式近端算子的简单函数，需要一种能处理无法精确计算近端算子的更复杂场景的采样方法。

Method: 提出iPULA算法，利用Moreau包络平滑势能，通过非精确近端计算来近似梯度评估，允许用可控的近似替代精确近端步骤。

Result: 建立了iPULA的非渐近收敛保证，明确刻画了非精确性对采样误差的影响，证明了非精确性在可量化偏差内保持收敛率。在医学图像重建任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: iPULA有效解决了非光滑复合势能场景下近端算子无法精确计算的问题，在理论上保证了收敛性，实践中在医学图像重建任务中表现良好。

Abstract: We study sampling from posterior distributions with nonsmooth composite potentials, a setting in which proximal-based Langevin methods are theoretically appealing but in practice limited to simple functions with closed-form proximal operators. We introduce iPULA for composite potentials, an inexact proximal unadjusted Langevin algorithm that replaces exact proximal steps with controlled approximations. Our approach leverages the Moreau envelope to smooth the potential, while allowing inexact evaluation of its gradient through inexact proximal computations. We establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for iPULA, explicitly characterizing the impact of inexactness on the sampling error and showing that the inexactness preserves convergence rates up to a quantifiable bias. We demonstrate the practical relevance of iPULA on a medical image reconstruction task, where proximal operators cannot be computed exactly. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of iPULA and support our theoretical results.

</details>


### [164] [Retractions by Alternating Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17384)
*Shixiang Chen,Yixiao He,Wen Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为光滑流形交集上的交替投影类方法建立了统一分析框架，证明了交替映射在清洁交条件下诱导出交流形上的收缩，且在更高光滑性下为二阶收缩，从而将多种具有二次/超线性收敛性的交替投影方案统一解释为二阶收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交替投影方法分析多局限于横截性等正则性条件下的局部收敛率研究，缺乏对更一般光滑流形交集中此类方法的统一理论框架。

Method: 在$C^{2,1}$嵌入子流形清洁交条件下，定义交替映射并证明其存在局部极限映射$\psi$，证明$\psi$是交流形上的收缩；在$C^{3,1}$条件下提升为二阶收缩。将多种交替投影类方案统一纳入该框架。

Result: 交替映射在清洁交条件下有定义良好的局部极限映射，该映射是交流形上的收缩；更高光滑性下为二阶收缩；多种具有二次/超线性收敛性的交替投影方案可被视为二阶收缩。

Conclusion: 该框架为流形交集约束优化问题提供了新的基于收缩的优化工具，统一了现有交替投影方法的理论分析。

Abstract: Alternating projections and their variants are classical tools for computing points in intersections of sets. Existing analyses for smooth manifolds mainly focus on local convergence rates under transversality or related regularity conditions. In this work, we develop a unified framework for a broad class of (possibly inexact) alternating-projection-type methods on intersections of smooth manifolds. Specifically, under the assumption that two $C^{2,1}$ embedded submanifolds $\mathcal{M}_1, \mathcal{M}_2 \subset \mathbb{R}^n$ intersect cleanly, we show that the associated alternating mapping admits a well-defined local limiting map $ψ$ on the intersection manifold $\mathcal{M}=\mathcal{M}_1\cap \mathcal{M}_2$, and that $ψ$ is a retraction on $\mathcal{M}$. If, in addition, $\mathcal{M}_1$ and $\mathcal{M}_2$ are $C^{3,1}$, then $ψ$ is a second-order retraction. Furthermore, several alternating-projection-type schemes that exhibit quadratic or superlinear local behavior under transversality can be understood as inducing second-order retractions on $\mathcal{M}$. This framework thus provides new retraction-based optimization tools for problems constrained to the intersection manifold.

</details>


### [165] [Stochastic Zeroth-Order Optimization Under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17394)
*Taha El Bakkali,El Mahdi Chayti,Qiuyi Zhang,Imane Rahali,Omar Saadi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出RSC-ZO方法，针对重尾噪声下的非凸随机零阶优化，在弱L_p条件下实现了与一阶方法最优ε依赖相匹配的高概率收敛界，且对于p=2时达到Õ(dε^{-4})，同时允许无限方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中梯度噪声常违反经典的有限方差假设（如重尾噪声），而现有非凸零阶优化在高概率保证下的理论分析尚不充分，且零阶优化无法直接沿用一阶方法的重尾理论，需要先将梯度噪声的弱L_p控制转移到标量方向估计上。

Method: 提出Robust Scalar-Clipped Zeroth-Order method (RSC-ZO)，一种两点零阶方法，在聚合前对每个标量方向导数进行裁剪，并分析其动量变体及批次大小/步长的权衡。

Result: 在样本级光滑性和梯度噪声的弱L_p尾条件下，RSC-ZO以高概率找到ε-驻点，复杂度为Õ(d^{p/(2(p-1))} ε^{-(3p-2)/(p-1)})，匹配最优一阶方法的ε依赖性。p=2时复杂度为Õ(dε^{-4})，匹配经典零阶维度-精度依赖，但具有高概率保证且允许无限方差。

Conclusion: RSC-ZO在重尾噪声下实现了非凸零阶优化的最优高概率收敛界，弥合了零阶与一阶方法在重尾噪声理论上的差距。

Abstract: We study stochastic zeroth-order (ZO) optimization of smooth nonconvex objectives under heavy-tailed sample-gradient noise. This regime is motivated by empirical evidence that gradient noise in modern machine learning can violate the bounded-variance assumptions used in classical ZO theory. While first-order methods have optimal rates under bounded $p$-th moment noise for $p\in(1,2]$, analogous high-probability guarantees for nonconvex ZO methods are much less understood.
  The ZO setting is not a direct corollary of first-order theory. First-order methods observe stochastic gradients, whereas derivative-free methods only query noisy function values and build finite-difference estimates. Thus, weak-$L_p$ control of $\nabla F(x;ξ)-\nabla f(x)$ must first be transferred to scalar directional estimates.
  We propose the Robust Scalar-Clipped Zeroth-Order method (RSC-ZO), a two-point method that clips each scalar directional derivative before aggregation. Under sample-wise smoothness and a weak-$L_p$ tail condition on the sample-gradient noise, RSC-ZO finds an $\varepsilon$-stationary point with high probability using $$ \widetilde{O}\!\left( d^{\frac{p}{2(p-1)}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}} \right) $$ noisy function evaluations. This matches the optimal first-order $\varepsilon$-dependence. At $p=2$, the bound becomes $\widetilde{O}(d\varepsilon^{-4})$, matching the classical stochastic ZO dimension--accuracy dependence, but with a high-probability guarantee and under a weaker weak-$L_2$ condition that can allow infinite variance. We also analyze a momentum variant and quantify its batch-size/stepsize tradeoff.

</details>


### [166] [Fully Discrete High-Order DG Scheme for Waves: Dispersion and Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17464)
*Yunzhang Li,Xiaoyang Wang,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了P^k局部间断Galerkin方法全离散逼近一维波动方程的谱结构、数值色散与可观性。揭示了耦合时空数值色散中的捕获机制，导致物理和伪模态的群速度在某些频率处消失，可观性常数出现exp(h^{-(1-ε)})指数爆炸。提出谱滤波策略恢复一致可观性，高阶方法可保留更宽物理频带，降低滤波成本与观测时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全离散局部间断Galerkin方法逼近一维波动方程时的谱结构、数值色散与可观性，揭示数值色散导致的捕获机制及其对可观性常数的影响。

Method: 采用P^k局部间断Galerkin方法进行全离散逼近，分析耦合时空数值色散特性，提出谱滤波策略以恢复一致可观性。

Result: 发现捕获机制导致可观性常数出现exp(h^{-(1-ε)})指数爆炸；提出谱滤波策略可恢复一致可观性；高阶方法能保留更宽物理频带，降低滤波成本与观测时间。

Conclusion: 谱滤波策略可克服捕获机制导致的发散问题，高阶方法在此恢复过程中更具优势。

Abstract: This paper investigates the spectral structure, numerical dispersion, and observability of fully discrete approximations of the one-dimensional wave equation by $P^k$ (local) discontinuous Galerkin methods. Characterizing the coupled space-time numerical dispersion reveals a trapping mechanism that forces the group velocities of both physical and spurious modes to vanish at selected frequencies. We then establish an exponential blow-up of order $\exp(h^{-(1-\varepsilon)})$ for the observability constant under this trapping mechanism. To overcome this divergence for arbitrary $k$, we propose a spectral filtering strategy to restore uniform observability. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments indicate that higher-order methods may facilitate this recovery by preserving a larger genuine physical frequency band, thereby reducing filtering cost and observation time.

</details>


### [167] [Port-Hamiltonian Control and Structure-Preserving Algorithm for Grid-Forming SVGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17487)
*Jiaxin Qian,Feng Ji,Sixu Wu,Mingyang Liu,Yifa Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对构网型静止无功发生器(SVG)提出了端口哈密顿(PH)建模、控制与保结构仿真框架，包括PH模型建立、输入-状态稳定(ISS)控制器设计以及Dirac结构保持中点格式，数值验证表明该方法在性能上优于传统PI控制器和标准龙格-库塔方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构网型静止无功发生器提供一套系统的端口哈密顿建模、控制与保结构仿真框架，解决传统方法在能量守恒、抗干扰能力和长期仿真精度方面的不足。

Method: 1) 建立SVG的端口哈密顿模型，捕获电感、电容和直流母线储能端口间的能量交换；2) 设计输入-状态稳定(ISS)控制器，仅含三个可调参数且物理意义明确，最小化扰动影响；3) 开发Dirac保结构中点规则，在无扰动时精确守恒哈密顿能量。

Result: ISS控制器相比传统PI控制器具有更快的调节速度、更小的稳态偏差和更低的控制代价；保结构中点规则实现精确能量守恒，长期精度优于标准龙格-库塔方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的PH框架在能量保持、抗扰动性能和长期仿真精度方面具有显著优势，为构网型SVG的高性能控制与仿真提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a port-Hamiltonian (PH) modeling, control, and structure-preserving simulation framework for grid-forming static var generators (SVGs). A PH model is established that captures energy exchange among the inductor, capacitor, and DC-link storage ports. Since external disturbances cannot be fully canceled by feedback, an input-to-state stable (ISS) controller is designed to steer subsystem states to zero while minimizing disturbance effects. The controller contains only three tunable parameters with clear physical interpretations and is robust against input errors. A Dirac-structure-preserving midpoint rule is developed, which exactly conserves the Hamiltonian energy when disturbances are absent. Numerical comparisons show that the ISS controller achieves faster settling, smaller offset, and lower control effort than a conventional PI controller, and the structure-preserving midpoint rule maintains exact energy conservation and superior long-term accuracy over standard Runge-Kutta methods.

</details>


### [168] [Inexact Adjoint Gradients and Directional Tolerances for Full-Potential Airfoil Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17599)
*Humberto Gimenes Macedo,Luís Felipe Bueno*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将离散伴随梯度误差分析与使用方向误差容限的优化方法相结合的理论框架，并将其应用于基于全势流求解器的翼型形状优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化方法在处理伴随梯度误差时缺乏系统性的容限控制，导致计算效率与收敛保证之间存在矛盾。本文旨在建立严格的误差传播理论，使优化过程能够在可控梯度误差下仍保持收敛性。

Method: 理论方面：推导了标量目标函数受状态方程约束的简化离散伴随公式，分析了状态残差和伴随残差非精确求解对简化梯度的误差传播，证明了梯度误差可由状态和伴随残差容限的线性组合上界控制，进而推导出方向容限条件使非精确梯度满足精确下降不等式。计算方法：使用抛物线初始网格生成器、椭圆网格平滑器、带人工密度稳定的全势离散化和近似因子分解迭代。

Result: 建立了残差与梯度误差之间的定量关系，证明了在紧致决策变量集上该上界具有一致性，并得到了非精确梯度满足精确下降不等式的方向容限条件。所提出的非精确广义方向法在均匀有界、递减和Armijo型步长规则下具有收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将离散伴随梯度误差分析与方向容限优化方法统一，为基于CFD的气动形状优化提供了理论保证和可行计算框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a framework connecting discrete adjoint gradient-error analysis with an optimization method that uses directional error tolerances, and applies it to airfoil shape optimization governed by a conservative full-potential flow solver on body-fitted structured meshes. The theoretical part derives the reduced discrete adjoint formula for scalar objectives constrained by a state equation and analyzes how inexact state and adjoint residuals propagate into the reduced gradient. For residuals that are affine in the state variable, the gradient error is bounded by a linear combination of the state and adjoint residual tolerances. On compact sets of decision variables, a uniform version of this bound is obtained, leading to a directional tolerance condition under which the inexact gradient satisfies an exact descent inequality. The resulting inexact general directions method inherits convergence properties under uniformly bounded, diminishing, and Armijo-type step-size rules. The computational part combines a parabolic initial grid generator, an elliptic mesh smoother, and a full-potential discretization with artificial-density stabilization and approximate-factorization iteration. The optimization problem is formulated as a pressure-matching problem in which a class-shape-transformation airfoil parametrization is adjusted so that the computed surface pressure coefficient approaches prescribed reference data, subject to mesh-generation and full-potential residual constraints.

</details>


### [169] [Training Infinitely Deep and Wide Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17660)
*Raphaël Barboni,Maarten V. de Hoop,Takashi Furuya,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Transformer在平均场（深度和宽度趋于无穷）下的训练动力学理论框架，将Transformer训练建模为神经PDE控制问题，建立了前向传播的适定性，推导了条件Wasserstein梯度公式，证明了梯度流的唯一存在性，并给出了注意力机制NTK核单射的充要条件（log-sum-exp函数族线性无关），在该条件下证明了梯度流收敛到全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer已成为现代机器学习的主导架构，但其训练动力学的理论基础仍然有限。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为梯度训练的Transformer提供严格的数学框架。

Method: 在平均场极限下，将Transformer训练建模为神经PDE控制问题（区别于ResNet的神经ODE），采用两种测度表示（逐层token分布与每层注意力参数分布），利用流映射和伴随灵敏度分析推导条件Wasserstein梯度显式公式，并研究NK核注意力机制的单射性条件。

Result: 建立了无穷深度Transformer前向传播的适定性；推导了训练风险的条件Wasserstein梯度的显式公式；证明了条件Wasserstein度量空间中梯度流曲线的存在唯一性；给出了注意力机制NTK核单射的充要条件（log-sum-exp函数族线性无关）；在该条件下证明了初始损失足够小时梯度流收敛到全局最小值。

Conclusion: 本文为梯度训练的Transformer提供了严格的平均场理论基础，揭示了Transformer与ResNet的本质区别（PDE vs ODE），并证明了在一定条件下优化景观中不存在虚假局部最小值，梯度流可收敛至全局最优。

Abstract: Transformers have become the dominant architecture in modern machine learning, yet the theoretical understanding of their training dynamics remains limited. This paper develops a rigorous mathematical framework for analyzing gradient-based training of transformers in the mean-field regime, where both the depth (number of layers) and width (number of attention heads) tend to infinity. While ResNet training can be understood as controlling a neural ODE, transformer training corresponds to controlling a neural PDE, due to the coupling of multiple token distributions through the attention mechanism. Our mean-field model features two types of measure representations: token distributions evolving through layers and attention parameters at each layer. We establish well-posedness of the forward pass through infinitely deep transformers, characterizing token evolution via flow maps that satisfy ODEs in function spaces. Using adjoint sensitivity analysis, we derive an explicit formula for the conditional Wasserstein gradient of the training risk, involving adjoint variables governed by backward ODEs. We prove the existence and uniqueness of gradient flow curves in the conditional Wasserstein metric space, establishing a rigorous foundation for gradient-based transformer training. A key technical contribution is providing necessary and sufficient conditions for injectivity of the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) for attention mechanisms: we show that NTK injectivity is equivalent to linear independence of log-sum-exp functions modulo affine functions, a condition satisfied by diverse token distributions, including discrete distributions, uniform distributions, and Gaussian mixtures. Under this NTK injectivity assumption, we prove that gradient flow converges to global minima when the initial loss is sufficiently small, eliminating spurious local minima from the optimization landscape.

</details>


### [170] [A reflected forward-backward splitting algorithmic framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17731)
*Haowen Zheng,Yongyu Fu,Qiao-Li Dong,Shuangbao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种反射式前向-后向分裂算法框架，用于求解有限个单调算子（包括极大单调算子、余强制算子以及单调且Lipschitz连续算子）之和的零点问题，在温和条件下提供了统一的收敛性分析，并通过数值实验提出了矩阵选择的启发式策略，最终在正则化鞍点问题上验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分裂算法在处理多个单调算子之和的零点问题时，往往需要针对每种算法单独进行收敛性分析，缺乏统一的框架。本文旨在提出一个通用的反射式前向-后向分裂算法框架，以统一分析多类单调算子的收敛性，并降低算法设计与分析的复杂度。

Method: 1. 提出反射式前向-后向分裂算法框架，涵盖极大单调算子、余强制算子以及单调且Lipschitz连续算子。2. 在温和条件下给出统一的收敛性分析，避免了对每个算法单独分析。3. 通过数值实验探索矩阵选择的启发式策略。4. 基于启发式策略推导出新算法，并在正则化鞍点问题上进行数值验证。

Result: 提出的反射式前向-后向分裂算法框架在温和条件下实现了统一的收敛性分析；数值实验提供了矩阵选择的启发式策略；基于该策略的新算法在正则化鞍点问题上表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的反射式前向-后向分裂算法框架为求解多类单调算子之和的零点问题提供了一种统一且有效的途径，矩阵选择的启发式策略有助于实际应用中的算法设计。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a reflected forward-backward splitting algorithic framework for finding a zero of the sum of finitely many monotone op-erators, including maximally monotone operators, cocoercive operators, and monotone and Lipschitz continuous operators. We provide a unified convergence analysis under mild conditions, eliminating the need to analyze the convergence of each algorithm individually. The heuristic strategies for matrix selections are proposed through a numerical experiment, based on which a new algorithm is derived. A further numerical experiment on the regularized saddle-point problem is then presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [171] [From Optimization to Satisficing: Robust Screening under Distributional Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17741)
*Shumin Ma,Daniel Zhuoyu Long,Lijian Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein度量的鲁棒满足（RS）框架，用于解决卖家在不确定买方估值分布下的鲁棒筛选问题。与传统鲁棒优化（RO）最大化最坏情况收入不同，RS框架通过最小化最坏情况下的收入缺口来达到指定收入目标，允许使用随机定价策略，并证明在买家剩余和公平性方面优于RO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒优化（RO）方法在分布不确定下优先最大化最坏情况收入，但存在两点不足：一是结果偏向卖家利益，二是高度依赖精确的模糊集设定。本文旨在提出一种替代框架，克服这些局限性。

Method: 提出鲁棒满足（Robust Satisficing, RS）框架，以Wasserstein距离衡量真实分布与参考分布之间的偏差，目标是在所有可能分布下最小化最坏情况收入缺口（shortfall），确保达到预设收入目标。采用随机定价策略，并与简单统一定价机制进行对比。

Result: RS框架具有良好的可处理性；简单统一定价在低目标水平和右偏估值分布下表现有效；当参考分布具有递增风险率时，RS比RO更稳定地提高买家剩余；当真实估值右偏时，RS提高卖家的样本外收入。

Conclusion: 以目标驱动的RS框架增强了买家剩余和公平性，为低估值买家提供更多机会，在估值需求偏向低端时可能提升整体收入。RS是对传统RO方法的实用替代，克服了模糊集校准的困难，同时确保更广泛的公平接入。

Abstract: This study investigates a robust screening problem under distributional ambiguity, where a seller is uncertain about a buyer's true valuation distribution, knowing only that it lies near a reference distribution measured by the Wasserstein metric. Traditional robust optimization (RO) approaches prioritize maximizing worst-case revenue within predefined ambiguity sets, often yielding seller-centric outcomes and reliance on precise set specifications. We propose a robust satisficing (RS) framework aimed at attaining a specified revenue target by minimizing the worst-case shortfall across all potential distributions. Our approach offers a tractable formulation and detailed characterization of optimal mechanisms using randomized pricing strategies. We also assess the out-of-sample efficacy of a simple posted pricing mechanism, finding it particularly effective with lower targets and positively skewed valuations, where smaller valuations have high probability mass. Comparing RO with RS, we find that RS consistently enhances buyer surplus when the reference distribution has an increasing hazard rate and increases out-of-sample seller revenue with positively skewed true valuations. Our analysis indicates that a target-driven RS framework enhances buyer surplus and fairness by offering more opportunities to lower-valuation buyers, potentially boosting overall revenue in scenarios with demand skewed toward these valuations. This approach offers a practical and viable modeling alternative to conventional RO methods, effectively overcoming the challenges of ambiguity set calibration while ensuring broader equitable access for diverse buyers.

</details>


### [172] [Convergence Analysis via ODE Approach for Convex Optimization with Linear Equality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17819)
*Chise Ishii,Yasushi Narushima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性约束凸优化问题中原始-对偶算法的连续时间动力学，通过Lyapunov函数提供定量收敛分析，将Nesterov加速方法推广到非光滑非强凸目标函数，基于KŁ性质建立O(1/t²)局部收敛率，并通过ODE离散化构建数值算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏和低秩模型的广泛应用使得大规模非光滑目标函数的凸优化问题日益重要，现有经典加速方法难以直接适用于这类问题，需要更通用的理论框架和收敛性分析。

Method: 基于ODE方法，将Nesterov加速方法的分析框架扩展到含线性等式约束的优化问题；对目标函数施加KŁ几何条件；通过Lyapunov函数进行定量收敛分析；通过离散化ODE构建数值算法方案。

Result: 建立了依赖局部几何性质的显式收敛速率，证明了O(1/t²)的局部收敛率；分析了算法参数的影响并给出了最优选择建议；数值实验验证了理论结果的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效将经典加速方法拓展到更广泛的大规模非光滑非强凸优化问题，所建立的收敛率与局部几何条件相关联，为实际算法参数调优提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies the continuous-time dynamics of primal-dual algorithms for linearly constrained convex optimization problems and provides a quantitative convergence analysis using the Lyapunov functions. With the growing prevalence of sparse and low-rank models, large-scale problems involving nonsmooth objective functions have become increasingly important. Our approach addresses nonsmooth and nonstrong convex objective functions, which is particularly effective in extending classical accelerated methods to broader large-scale optimization problems. Building upon the ordinary differential equation (ODE) approach inspired by the recent work on Nesterov's acceleration methods, we extend the analysis to an ODE associated with an optimization problem with linear equality constraints. Moreover, by imposing a geometric condition analogous to the \textit{Kurdyka--\textŁojasiewicz (K\textŁ) property} on the objective function, we derive convergence rates that depend explicitly on the local geometry and establish the $\mathcal{O}(1/t^2)$ local convergence rate. For the algorithmic construction, a numerical scheme is derived by discretizing the proposed ODE. Furthermore, we investigate the influence of algorithm parameters and provide insights into their optimal selection. Finally, preliminary numerical experiments are provided to validate the consistency with the theoretical results.

</details>


### [173] [On the convergence of doubly stochastic Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17883)
*Yiheng Xiao,Huikang Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双随机原始-对偶混合梯度方法（DSPDHG），用于求解具有可分离结构的凸-凹鞍点问题。该方法同时在原始和对偶变量上执行随机块更新，统一了确定性PDHG和随机PDHG（SPDHG），并证明了O(1/K)的遍历收敛率以及在二次增长条件下的线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模应用中，对原始变量或对偶变量进行全更新可能计算代价过高，现有方法要么是确定性的（PDHG），要么仅在一侧随机化（SPDHG），缺乏同时在两侧进行随机块更新的统一框架。

Method: 提出DSPDHG算法，在每次迭代中对原始变量和对偶变量都进行随机块采样更新，通过块状步长条件保证收敛。同时设计了重启变体，在二次增长条件下实现线性收敛。

Result: 在一般凸设置下建立了O(1/K)的遍历收敛率（期望意义下的受限原始-对偶间隙）；在二次增长条件下证明了重启外迭代的线性收敛率；数值实验表明重启DSPDHG在标准步长下性能优于PDHG、SPDHG及其重启变体。

Conclusion: DSPDHG为块结构凸-凹鞍点问题提供了一种灵活高效的双随机优化框架，统一了确定性方法和单侧随机方法，并在理论和实验上均展现了良好的收敛性能。

Abstract: We study a block-structured class of convex-concave saddle-point problems in which both the primal and dual variables admit natural separable decompositions. Motivated by large-scale applications where a full update on either side can be computationally expensive, we propose a doubly stochastic primal--dual hybrid gradient method (DSPDHG) that performs randomized block updates on both primal and dual variables.The method extends classical PDHG and stochastic PDHG (SPDHG) schemes in a unified manner:it reduces to deterministic PDHG when all blocks are selected and to one-sided stochastic variants when only one side is randomized. For the general convex setting, we establish an $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$ ergodic convergence rate for the expected restricted primal--dual gap under suitable blockwise step-size conditions. We further analyze a restarted variant of DSPDHG under a quadratic growth condition in terms of the smoothed primal-dual gap. Under this regularity assumption, we prove linear convergence of the restarted outer iterates. Numerical evidence is provided to show that restarted DSPDHG with standard step sizes demonstrates competitive practical performance compared with PDHG, SPDHG, and their restarted variants.

</details>


### [174] [A Differentiable Interior-Point Method in Single Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17913)
*Jon Arrizabalaga,Kevin Tracy,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于低精度（单精度）算术的可微分内点法，通过替代互补性表示确保线性系统在解附近保持谱有界，从而能够在单精度下可靠求解和微分约束优化问题，而此前这类问题仅限于双精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准原对偶内点法在解附近会出现线性系统病态问题，这在双精度下尚可接受，但在单精度下会引发灾难性后果，导致无法充分利用现代机器学习中加速硬件的潜力。因此需要一种能在低精度下稳定运行的可微分内点法。

Method: 采用替代互补性表示（alternative complementarity representation），确保底层线性系统在解附近保持谱有界，从而避免病态问题；在此基础上构建可微分内点法，支持单精度算术下的求解和梯度计算。

Result: 所提方法使内点求解器能够在单精度下可靠求解和微分优化问题，而标准内点公式在单精度下会失败。通过消融实验与标准内点法的对比，以及在双层学习和端到端学习场景中的应用展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了低精度可微优化求解器的空白，使内点法能够充分利用单精度加速硬件，在需要微分约束优化的场景（如双层优化、端到端学习）中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Primal-dual interior-point methods solve constrained convex optimization problems to tight tolerances with speed and robustness. Their solutions are also efficiently differentiable with respect to the problem data through the implicit function theorem. However, the standard treatment of primal-dual complementarity makes the underlying linear systems increasingly ill-conditioned near the solution. While this ill-conditioning is often benign in double precision, it can be catastrophic in single precision, preventing interior-point methods from fully exploiting the accelerated hardware that underpins modern machine learning. This paper introduces a differentiable interior-point method designed for low-precision arithmetic. By using an alternative complementarity representation, we ensure that the underlying linear systems remain spectrally bounded -- even near the solution -- a property that is essential for computing accurate gradients and avoiding arithmetic exceptions. As a result, our method enables interior-point solvers to reliably solve and differentiate optimization problems in single precision that were previously confined to double precision. We demonstrate the approach through an ablation study against the standard interior-point formulation and applications in bilevel and end-to-end learning settings where differentiating through constrained optimization is essential. The source code is available at https://github.com/qpax-solver/qpax.

</details>


### [175] [Quantitative Fredholm backstepping and rapid stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17941)
*Ludovick Gagnon,Amaury Hayat,Swann Marx,Shengquan Xiang,Christophe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对自伴和斜伴算子A，证明了存在Fredholm反步变换，克服了经典框架的两大局限，给出了变换T及其逆的显式定量估计，并获得了定量快速镇定和小时间零能控性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服经典Fredholm反步框架的两大局限性，特别是避免传统文献中使用的紧性论证和Riesz基机制，为高阶算子建立更完善的变换存在性理论。

Method: 显式识别了构造变换T所用的同构结构，绕过紧性论证和Riesz基机制，直接推导出变换及其逆算子范数关于衰减率λ的定量尖锐估计。

Result: 获得了‖T‖和‖T⁻¹‖关于衰减率λ的定量尖锐估计，进而得到一大类算子的定量快速镇定和小时间零能控性结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的显式Fredholm反步变换框架不仅解决了高阶自伴/斜伴算子的变换存在性问题，还提供了定量估计，为相关控制理论问题提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the existence of Fredholm backstepping transformations for self-adjoint and skew-adjoint operators $A$. Under suitable assumptions on the operator $A$ and the possibly unbounded control operator $B$, we prove the existence of a Fredholm backstepping transformation for operators of order strictly greater than $1$.
  This work overcomes two major limitations of the classical Fredholm backstepping framework. One of the main contributions is the explicit identification of the underlying isomorphism used in the construction of the transformation $T$, thereby bypassing the compactness arguments and Riesz basis mechanisms traditionally used in the literature. This explicit structure enables us to derive quantitative and sharp estimates for $\|T\|_{\mathcal{L}(H;H)}$ and $\|T^{-1}\|_{\mathcal{L}(H;H)}$ with respect to the decay rate $λ$. As a consequence, we obtain quantitative rapid stabilization and small-time null controllability results for a broad class of operators.

</details>


### [176] [From Saddle Points Toward Global Minima: A Newton-Type Method on Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17963)
*Razvan-Andrei Lascu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Saddle-Free Newton (WSFN)方法，一种在Wasserstein空间上用于非凸泛函极小化的二阶优化算法。该方法通过正则化平方根形式的Wasserstein Hessian预条件处理Wasserstein梯度，在保留正曲率方向吸引力的同时诱导负曲率方向的排斥力，从而克服标准Wasserstein Newton动力学易被鞍点吸引的问题。论文建立了Wasserstein空间上严格局部极小性的二阶充分最优性条件，证明了WSFN在良性景观假设下能在多项式时间内逃离鞍点区域并达到全局极小点的α邻域，且对鞍点参数的依赖优于现有扰动一阶方法。进入邻域后，WSFN以线性速率收敛到非退化全局极小点。最后给出了基于粒子的实现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Wasserstein梯度方法主要是一阶方法，虽能避开良性景观的鞍点，但缺乏局部快速收敛性。二阶Wasserstein Newton动力学虽能提供快速收敛，却易被鞍点吸引。需要一种既能逃离鞍点又能快速局部收敛的二阶方法。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Saddle-Free Newton (WSFN)方法，通过正则化平方根形式的Wasserstein Hessian预条件Wasserstein梯度，实现沿正曲率方向吸引、沿负曲率方向排斥的动力学。建立了Wasserstein空间上严格局部极小性的二阶充分最优性条件。

Result: 在良性景观假设下，WSFN能在多项式时间内逃离鞍点并达到全局极小点的α邻域，对鞍点参数的依赖优于一阶扰动方法。进入邻域后，WSFN在L^2-Wasserstein距离下线性收敛到非退化全局极小点。

Conclusion: WSFN成功将鞍点逃逸能力与局部快速收敛性统一于二阶Wasserstein优化框架，理论分析证明其全局收敛性和局部线性收敛率，粒子实现方法表明算法具有实际可行性。

Abstract: We study the minimization of non-convex functionals over the Wasserstein space. While recent work has showed that perturbed Wasserstein gradient methods can avoid saddle points for benign landscapes, existing approaches remain essentially first-order and do not provide fast local convergence once the iterates enter a neighborhood of a global minimizer. We propose Wasserstein Saddle-Free Newton (WSFN), a second-order method that preconditions the Wasserstein gradient by a regularized square root of the squared Wasserstein Hessian. This construction preserves attraction toward directions of positive curvature while inducing repulsion along directions of negative curvature, thereby overcoming the tendency of standard Wasserstein Newton dynamics to be attracted to saddles. We also establish second-order sufficient optimality conditions on Wasserstein space for strict local minimality. Under regularity and benign landscape assumptions, we prove that WSFN escapes saddle regions and reaches an $α$-neighborhood of a global minimizer in polynomial time, with improved dependence on saddle parameters compared with prior perturbed first-order methods. Once inside this neighborhood, we show that WSFN converges linearly in $L^2$-Wasserstein distance to a non-degenerate global minimizer. Finally, we present a particle-based implementation of the method.

</details>


### [177] [Control-Channel Informativity for Koopman EDMDc under Behavior-Policy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17966)
*Yue Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展动态模式分解与控制（EDMDc）在行为策略或预存反馈控制器生成的轨迹训练中，因控制通道信息不足导致无法识别新输入命令对提升状态影响的问题。提出了条件干预证书（即投影输入数据后残差输入协方差的Schur补），证明了其严格正定是有限样本下提升控制通道块可辨识性的充要条件，并给出了闭环统计界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDMDc常由行为策略或预存反馈控制器生成的轨迹训练，这类数据虽能准确预测观察到的行为，但无法识别新输入命令如何改变提升状态。本文将这一失败建模为控制通道信息性问题，旨在量化何时数据足以辨识控制通道动态。

Method: 引入条件干预证书，定义为输入数据投影到活跃提升状态特征张成空间后的残差输入协方差（即EDMDc信息矩阵中提升状态块的Schur补）。证明其严格正定是有限样本下提升控制通道块可辨识性的充要条件；当证书为零时，不同提升模型在收集的转移上一致但在反事实输入下不一致。随后利用可预测回归子、条件次高斯转移噪声和正则化Schur补给出闭环统计界。

Result: 在标量反馈示例下，抖动反馈中残差干预信息随抖动幅度平方增长，控制通道误差随干预信噪比倒数尺度下降。线性系统实验精确验证了这些标度律，受控Duffing和Van der Pol基准实验也呈现诊断性标度。更大的EDMDc采集网格表明，状态覆盖、联合回归条件和干预激励是互补的诊断指标而非可互换的性能度量。

Conclusion: 条件干预证书严格正定是EDMDc控制通道可辨识性的关键条件。状态覆盖、联合回归条件与干预激励是互补的而非可互换的；单纯扩大采集网格不能替代充分的干预激励。

Abstract: Extended dynamic mode decomposition with control (EDMDc) is often trained from trajectories generated by a behavior policy or a pre-existing feedback controller. Such data can predict the observed behavior accurately while failing to identify how new input commands change the lifted state. This paper studies that failure as a control-channel informativity problem. We introduce a conditional intervention certificate, defined as the residual input covariance after projecting the input data away from the active lifted-state feature span. The certificate is the Schur complement of the lifted-state block in the EDMDc information matrix. We prove that its strict positivity is necessary and sufficient for finite-sample sample- identifiability of the lifted control-channel block. If the certificate vanishes, distinct lifted models agree on every collected transition but disagree under counterfactual inputs. We then give a closed-loop statistical bound using predictable regressors, conditionally sub-Gaussian transition noise, and a regularized Schur complement. A scalar feedback example shows the unavoidable scaling: under dithered feedback, residual intervention information grows quadratically with the dither amplitude and the control-channel error decreases with the inverse intervention signal-to-noise scale. New experiments verify these scalings exactly in a linear system and diagnostically in controlled Duffing and Van der Pol benchmarks. A larger EDMDc acquisition grid further shows that state coverage, joint regression conditioning, and intervention excitation are complementary diagnostics rather than interchangeable performance score.

</details>


### [178] [Robust Optimal Reinsurance, Investment,and Surplus Allocation for Epstein-Zin Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18145)
*Junyi Guo,Jianxuan Li,Qianqian Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完全市场中具有Epstein-Zin递归偏好的保险人的稳健最优再保险、投资和内部盈余分配（消费）问题，推导了单位和非单位跨期替代弹性情形下的显式解，并与非稳健解及Campbell-Shiller近似解进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保险公司的实际运营中，再保险、投资和消费决策面临模型不确定性和市场不完全性。现有研究多基于期望效用理论或忽略模型不确定性，该文旨在引入Epstein-Zin递归偏好以分离风险厌恶和跨期替代弹性，并在不完全市场下求解稳健最优策略。

Method: 采用经典动态规划方法，在风险资产价格具有随机漂移率（由O-U过程驱动）的不完全市场假设下，分别针对单位EIS和非单位EIS两种情形推导稳健最优策略的显式解，并将其与非稳健解及Campbell-Shiller近似解进行对比。

Result: 获得了单位EIS和非单位EIS情形下最优稳健再保险、投资和消费策略的显式解，且稳健解与非稳健解的对比结果符合经济学直觉；同时与Campbell-Shiller近似方法的对比评估了近似解的精度。

Conclusion: 本文在不完全市场框架下为具有递归偏好的保险人提供了稳健最优策略的显式刻画，验证了模型不确定性对决策行为的重要影响，并确认了近似方法的适用范围与局限性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the robust optimal reinsurance,investment,and internal surplus distribution (i.e., consumption) problem for an insurer with Epstein-Zin recursive preferences in an incomplete market. It is assumed that the insurer can allocate wealth to a financial market consisting of a risk-free asset and a risky asset, where the price process of the risky asset follows a diffusion process with a stochastic drift rate governed by an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (O-U) process. For both the unit and non-unit elasticity of intertemporal substitution (EIS) cases, by applying the classical dynamic programming approach, we derive explicit solutions for the optimal robust reinsurance, investment,and consumption strategies and also verify that the obtained solutions indeed solve the optimal control problem. Furthermore, we compare the robust solutions with their non-robust counterparts, and the comparative results shown in the figures are consistent with economic intuition. Finally, we contrast the exact solutions with the Campbell-Shiller approximation and assess the accuracy of the approximation method.

</details>


### [179] [Resolvent Moreau identities without monotonicity: theory and applications to Gabay duality, Douglas--Rachford and ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18158)
*Andrew Calcan,Jordan Collard,Alberto De Marchi,Scott B. Lindstrom*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发现非凸函数之间仍存在一种新型对偶关系——不是函数本身，而是其预解算子之间存在类似对偶的关系，这是Moreau恒等式的推广。基于此对偶，作者研究了现有算子方案并设计了新方案，特别是将Gabay揭示的Douglas-Rachford分裂与ADMM之间的对偶关系扩展到非单调包含问题，并给出了ADMM在若干开放情形下发散的反例。进一步，作者设计了一类收敛的预解同伦方案，用于求解非凸正则化最小绝对偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对偶理论只适用于凸函数，对于非凸函数则失效。作者希望探索非凸情形下是否仍存在某种形式的对偶关系，并利用这种关系来设计新的算法、解决实际中重要的非凸优化问题（如非凸正则化LAD问题）。

Method: 证明任意集值映射的预解算子之间存在对偶关系（推广Moreau恒等式）；将Douglas-Rachford分裂与ADMM之间的对偶关系从单调情形扩展到非单调包含问题；利用DR方案更易分析的特点，构造ADMM发散的反例；设计收敛的预解同伦方案。

Result: 建立了非凸函数情形下预解算子的对偶关系；将DR-ADMM对偶推广到非单调情形；给出了ADMM在若干开放情形下的发散反例；提出了收敛的预解同伦方案。

Conclusion: 非凸函数之间虽无经典对偶，但其预解算子之间存在推广的对偶关系。该关系可用于理解、分析和设计优化算法，特别是解决非凸正则化LAD等强凸性缺失的难题。

Abstract: Duality is most often defined as a relationship between convex functions. If those functions are nonconvex, classical duality breaks down. Notwithstanding, we show that another kind of duality still exists, not between the functions themselves, but between the so-called resolvent operators used to solve associated problems. In fact, this duality-like relationship holds for any set-valued mapping, and is a generalization of the Moreau's identity. We use this duality to study existing operator schemes and to design new ones. In particular, we show that the duality-like relationship Daniel Gabay illuminated between the Douglas--Rachford splitting (DR) and the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) extends to nonmonotone inclusion problems. We use this relationship to provide explicit counterexamples to the convergence of ADMM in several open cases, by studying the (easier to analyse) DR scheme. Motivated by our observations, we design a class of convergent resolvent homotopy schemes and use them to solve nonconvex-regularised least absolute deviations problems. This important problem class has received little attention in the literature, since the convex component of the objective does not enjoy strong convexity.

</details>


### [180] [Trajectory convergence and $o(t^{-2})$ rates for Nesterov accelerated primal-dual dynamics without Lipschitz gradient assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18236)
*Xin He,Nan-Jing Hang,Yi-Bin Xiao,Ya-Ping Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Nesterov加速原始-对偶动力系统，在无需梯度Lipschitz连续假设的条件下，证明了有限维空间中轨迹收敛到原始-对偶解，并在α>3时建立了o(t^{-2})的收敛速率，首次处理了临界情况α=3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究(Boţ and Nguyen, 2021)在Hilbert框架下建立了该动力系统轨迹的弱收敛性，但要求α>3且∇f满足Lipschitz连续条件。本文旨在去除Lipschitz连续性假设，同时覆盖临界情况α=3，并建立更优的收敛速率。

Method: 采用Bregman距离论证替代传统依赖Lipschitz连续性的分析框架，通过构建合适的能量函数和Lyapunov分析，在有限维空间中建立轨迹的收敛性及收敛速率。该方法还可扩展至时间尺度变换的原始-对偶动力学系统。

Result: 在α≥3且无需∇f Lipschitz连续的条件下，证明了轨迹收敛到原始-对偶解；当α>3时，目标残差和可行性违规均达到o(t^{-2})的改进收敛速率；首次解决了临界情况α=3的轨迹收敛问题。

Conclusion: 本文去除了该领域长期依赖的Lipschitz梯度假设，推广了现有理论结果，并填补了加速原始-对偶动力系统在临界阻尼情况下的收敛性空白，为后续研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We consider the Nesterov accelerated primal-dual dynamical system \[ \begin{cases} \ddot{x}(t)+\dfracα{t}\dot{x}(t) +\nabla f(x(t)) +A^\top\bigl(λ(t)+θt\dotλ(t)\bigr)+βA^\top(Ax(t)-b)=0,\\[0.6em] \ddotλ(t)+\dfracα{t}\dotλ(t) -\bigl(A(x(t)+θt\dot{x}(t))-b\bigr)=0, \end{cases} \] which is linked to the linearly constrained optimization problem $
  \min_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n} f(x),\ s.t.\ Ax=b, $ where $α\ge 3$ and $f$ is convex and continuously differentiable. In a Hilbert framework, the weak convergence of its trajectory was established by Boţ and Nguyen (J. Differential Equations, 303:369--406, 2021) under $α>3$ and the Lipschitz continuity assumption on $\nabla f$. In this paper, we prove in finite-dimensional spaces that the trajectory converges to a primal-dual solution for $α\ge3$, without assuming Lipschitz continuity of $\nabla f$. Moreover, when $α>3$, we establish improved $o(t^{-2})$ convergence rates for both the objective residual and the feasibility violation. Our analysis relies on Bregman-distance arguments, instead of the Lipschitz continuity of $\nabla f$. The same strategy can also be extended to time-scaled primal-dual dynamics to obtain analogous convergence results. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first results in this topic without Lipschitz gradient assumption. Our result also present the first work on the convergence of the trajectory of the accelerated primal-dual dynamical system for the critical case $α=3$.

</details>


### [181] [Optimizing vaccine allocation in an age-structured SIR model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18256)
*Luís Almeida,Romain Ducasse,Elisa Paparelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究疫情爆发期间的最优疫苗分配问题，使用年龄结构SIR模型，证明动态优化问题等价于静态优化问题，并利用该等价性获得最优疫苗分配的定性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限疫苗供应约束下，寻找最优疫苗分配策略以最小化疫情造成的死亡人数，同时考虑人口异质性（年龄差异）对感染率和死亡率的影响。

Method: 采用年龄结构SIR模型描述疫情传播，将时变和年龄依赖的疫苗接种率作为控制函数，通过将动态优化问题转化为等价的静态优化问题来求解。

Result: 证明了原动态优化问题与某个静态优化问题等价，并利用此等价性推导出最优疫苗分配的定性性质。

Conclusion: 通过问题等价性转化，能够获得有限疫苗供应下最优分配策略的定性特征，为疫情管理提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem where the objective is to find the best vaccine allocation during an epidemic outbreak. The epidemic dynamics is described by an age-structured SIR model with nonlocal interactions. Both the infection and death rates depend on the age of the individuals, reflecting the effect of heterogeneities within the population. Our model includes a vaccination term, depending on time and age, which serves as a control function. The aim is to minimize the impact of the epidemic, that is, the number of casualties, under the constraint of limited vaccine supply. In a first part, we show that our optimization problem is equivalent to another static optimization problem. We then use this new optimization problem to obtain qualitative properties for the optimal allocations of vaccines.

</details>


### [182] [Null Controllability for Degenerate Parabolic Equations with Internal Control Applied on a Measurable Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18307)
*Donghui Yang,Mengze Gu,Baozhu Guo,Ghadir Shokor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文延续前期工作，通过分离变量法建立退化抛物方程的Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式，并证明其在可测子集上内控制下的零能控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为不同类型的退化抛物方程建立零能控性理论，并探索分离变量法在退化问题中的适用性，作为传统方法的替代途径。

Method: 采用分离变量法（separable variable method）推导Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式，并基于该不等式证明方程在可测子集上内控制下的零能控性。

Result: 成功建立了另一类退化抛物方程的Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式，并证明了其在可测子集上内控制下的零能控性。

Conclusion: 分离变量法可为退化抛物方程的可控性证明提供一种替代性方法。

Abstract: This work serves as a continuation of our preceding paper [28]. In that study, we presented a separable variable method to derive the Lebeau-Robbiano spectral inequality for a specific degenerate parabolic equation and subsequently employed it to demonstrate the null controllability of said equation when internal control is applied to an open subset. In the current paper, we reapply the separable variable method to attain the Lebeau-Robbiano spectral inequality for a different degenerate parabolic equation, and we substantiate the null controllability of this equation with internal control acting on a measurable subset. This approach may offer an alternative means of proving controllability results for degenerate parabolic equations.

</details>


### [183] [Attention-based PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18315)
*Rodrigo Maulen-Soto,Claire Boyer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过无监督PCA的视角研究注意力机制，证明在训练于高斯数据时，softmax和线性注意力层学习到的参数与协方差矩阵的主特征向量对齐，建立了注意力与PCA的直接联系。分析覆盖有限和无限提示场景，在无限提示极限下收敛到全局最优解，在有限提示设置中表现出相同行为（受采样效应影响）。进一步扩展到spiked Wishart协方差的情境学习，注意力成功恢复潜在信号方向。结果表明注意力在无监督目标下本质执行PCA类计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无监督学习（PCA）的视角理解注意力机制的工作原理，为注意力的表示学习能力提供理论基础。

Method: 理论分析：将softmax和线性注意力层训练与PCA建立显式联系；分析有限和无限提示两种场景；扩展到spiked Wishart协方差的情境学习设置。

Result: 注意力层参数与协方差矩阵主特征向量对齐；无限提示下收敛到全局最优；有限提示下受采样效应影响但行为一致；在spiked Wishart协方差下成功恢复信号方向。

Conclusion: 注意力机制在无监督目标下本质上执行PCA类计算，为其表示学习能力提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study attention mechanisms through the lens of a canonical unsupervised problem: principal component analysis (PCA). We show that, when trained on Gaussian data, both softmax and linear attention layers learn parameters that align with the principal eigenvectors of the covariance matrix, thereby establishing a direct and explicit connection with PCA. Our analysis covers both finite and infinite prompt regimes. In the infinite-prompt limit, we prove convergence to globally optimal solutions aligned with the leading spectral direction, while in the finiteprompt setting we show that the same behavior emerges up to sampling effects. We further extend the analysis to an in-context setting with spiked Wishart covariances, where attention successfully recovers the underlying signal direction. These results demonstrate that attention inherently performs PCA-like computations under unsupervised objectives, providing a theoretical foundation for its representation-learning capabilities.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum Model for CVRPTW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18393)
*Imran Meghazi,Éric Bourreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Grover Search框架的量子算法，用于求解带时间窗的容量受限车辆路径问题(CVRPTW)。采用"先规划路线，后分簇"的经典技术启发式模型，并在门级量子计算机上实现。针对NISQ时代量子比特数量受限的问题，引入了一种量子比特高效的分割启发式建模，仅向标准TSP量子公式添加线性数量的决策量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邮政服务等在包裹递送等时效性敏感场景中常面临带时间窗的容量受限车辆路径问题(CVRPTW)，经典"先路线后分簇"方法存在固有次优性，量子计算范式有望克服这一局限性。

Method: 基于Grover Search框架，采用受经典"route first, cluster second"技术启发的模型，在门级量子计算机上实现。针对NISQ时代量子比特数量限制，引入了一种量子比特高效的分割启发式建模方法，仅向标准TSP量子公式添加线性数量的决策量子比特。

Result: 所提出的量子比特高效建模方法能够以线性数量的额外决策量子比特处理时间窗和容量约束，相比标准TSP量子公式更具可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该量子算法在NISQ时代有望为解决CVRPTW提供新途径，通过克服经典分解方法的次优性，实现更优的路径规划方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a quantum algorithm for the capacitated vehicle routing problem with time windows (CVRPTW) based on Grover Search framework. This problem is often faced by Postal services in the context of package delivery or other time-sensitive operations. We provide an implementation on gate based quantum computer of a model inspired by classical route first, cluster second technique. The quantum paradigm allows to overcome suboptimality inherent property of this decomposition. In the current NISQ (Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum) era, the most important limitation is the number of available qubits which makes time windows and capacity constraints hard to tackle. We introduce a qubit-efficient split-inspired modeling which adds only a linear number of decision qubits to standard quantum formulations for Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP).

</details>


### [185] [Solving a large oral examination timetabling problem using a multidimensional knapsack MILP formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18402)
*Cyrille Briand,Jean-Pierre Belaud*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对法国工程师学校入学口试调度问题（OETP），将其建模为多维背包问题（MKP），采用混合整数线性规划（MILP）将候选人分配给预验证的日程方案，在20分钟内成功调度了7,796/7,804名考生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法国精英工程师学校入学口试涉及超过7,000名考生，需在四周内满足考试冲突、地理来源、签证要求等多重约束，手工调度极其复杂，亟需自动化优化方法。

Method: 将OETP建模为多维背包问题（MKP），采用混合整数线性规划（MILP）表述，通过将候选人分配至预验证的日程方案而非独立时间槽，降低组合复杂度。使用SCIP求解器在真实数据集上求解。

Result: 在2025年真实数据实例上，以20分钟为时间限制，成功调度7,804名候选人中的7,796名，仅8人未完成分配。

Conclusion: 所提出的MILP-MKP方法能有效处理大规模口试调度问题，在有限时间内获得高质量可行解，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Oral Examination Timetabling Problem (OETP) for France's prestigious engineering schools, an organization managed by the Service des Concours Communs Polytechniques (SCCP). The scheduling is highly complex, involving over 7,000 candidates across a four-week period while accounting for constraints such as exam overlaps, geographic origin, and visa requirements. To manage this scale, the paper shows how to model the problem as a Multidimensional Knapsack Problem (MKP) using a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation. Their strategy reduces combinatorial complexity by assigning candidates to a predetermined set of pre-validated schedules rather than individual time slots. Experimental results using the SCIP solver on a real 2025 data instance successfully accommodated 7,796 out of 7,804 candidates within a 20-minute time limit.

</details>


### [186] [The distance-based formation controller design for multi-agent systems in port-Hamiltonian form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18502)
*Jingyi Zhao,Yongxin Wu,Héctor García de Marina,Yuhu Wu,Yann Le Gorrec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对港口-哈密顿动力学的多智能体系统，本文提出了一种融合距离基编队控制与碰撞避免的统一控制器，通过构造有向无环图的符号关联矩阵、仅使用吸引势场并结合安全屏障，解决了局部极小值问题，并利用拉萨尔不变性原理保证了闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编队控制中的碰撞可能导致智能体损坏，现有基于人工势场的方法存在局部极小值问题，需要设计一种既能实现编队又能保证碰撞避免的统一控制策略。

Method: 构造对应有向无环图且满列秩的符号关联矩阵；设计仅包含吸引势场的人工势场，避免局部极小值；利用安全屏障严格强制执行碰撞避免；基于拉萨尔不变性原理进行稳定性分析。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提控制策略在速度跟踪、目标编队获取和智能体间安全性方面的有效性与可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一控制器能够同时管理速度跟踪、目标编队获取和智能体间安全，具有良好的稳定性保障。

Abstract: Based on the practical scenario where collisions in formation control may lead to agent damage, this paper investigates the integrated problem of distance-based formation control and collision avoidance for multi-agent systems governed by port-Hamiltonian dynamics.
