A foundational step involves constructing a signed incidence matrix, which, by design, corresponds to a directed acyclic graph and possesses the full column rank property. To overcome the prevalent issue of local minima in traditional artificial potential fields, a novel design utilizing attraction-only potentials is introduced, with collision avoidance rigorously enforced by safety barriers. This framework leads to a unified controller that concurrently manages velocity tracking, target formation acquisition, and inter-agent safety. The stability of the resulting closed-loop system is guaranteed through LaSalle's invariance principle. Numerical simulations demonstrate the validity and effectiveness of the proposed control strategy.

</details>


### [187] [Boundary Stabilization for the Rayleigh Beam System under Event-triggered Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18520)
*Siwen Wang,Yi Cheng,Yuhu Wu,Yongxin Wu,Wen Kang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Rayleigh梁系统的边界镇定问题，提出了两种结合事件触发机制的控制律，利用积分乘子技术和能量摄动法构造了参数确定的充分条件，保证了闭环系统的指数稳定性，并精确确定了指数衰减率。数值算例验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现Rayleigh梁系统的边界镇定，同时引入事件触发机制以减少控制更新的频率，提高控制效率。

Method: 提出了两种事件触发控制律；采用积分乘子技术和能量摄动法构造参数确定的充分条件。

Result: 所提事件触发控制能够保证闭环系统指数稳定，且指数衰减率可精确确定。

Conclusion: 事件触发控制方法对Rayleigh梁边界镇定有效，数值实验验证了其效果。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose two event-triggered control laws incorporating an eventtriggering mechanism to tackle the boundary stabilization for the Rayleigh beam system. Under this event-triggered controls, a sufficient condition for parameter determination is constructed to guarantee the exponential stability of the closed-loop system by using the integral multiplier technique and energy perturbation method, wherein the desired exponential decay rate can be precisely determined. Numerical examples are presented to demonstrate the efficacy of the event-triggered control methodology.

</details>


### [188] [Efficient Gradient Methods for Distributed Saddle Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18604)
*Ruichen Luo,Anton Rodomanov,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了分布式鞍点问题（SPs）的通信与计算成本，提出了一种基于多阶段归约到残差范数解耦最小化的新方法，在零尊重框架下实现了最优通信代价，并通过匹配下界证明该方法在梯度跨度算法族中是通信最优的。进一步将结果推广到变分不等式问题（VIP），取得了新的最优通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式鞍点问题在机器学习和多智能体系统中具有广泛应用，但其理论基础尚未完善建立，现有方法在通信效率上存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种解耦方法，通过多阶段归约将原问题转化为残差范数的解耦最小化问题，在零尊重框架下设计通信协议，并给出梯度跨度算法族中的匹配下界证明。

Result: 该方法在通信代价上优于已知最优结果及Extragradient方法的长期oracle代价；在梯度跨度算法族中达到通信最优；在变分不等式问题中实现了新的最优通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文为分布式鞍点问题提供了严格的理论基础，提出的解耦方法在多个维度上达到了通信最优，并成功推广到更一般的变分不等式问题，显著推动了该领域的理论发展。

Abstract: The distributed setting for Saddle Problems (SPs) has recently emerged as a framework for various modern applications in machine learning and multiagent systems. Despite its relevance, the theoretical foundations of this setting have not yet been thoroughly established. In this paper, we advance this research direction by formalizing the distributed setup for SPs and providing rigorous definitions of communication and computational costs. Our main result is a novel decoupled method that achieves optimal communication cost within the zero-respecting framework. Our method is based on a multi-stage reduction to the decoupled minimization of residual norms, which yields strict improvements over the best known communication cost for the class and the long-standing oracle cost of the Extragradient method. Further, we show by a matching lower bound that our method is communication-optimal within the family of gradient-span algorithms. Finally, we study the extension of distributed SP into Variational Inequality Problem (VIP), which generalizes two-player zero-sum games to multiplayer general-sum games. We show that our decoupled method achieves a new state-of-the-art communication complexity for this broader class.

</details>


### [189] [Singleton Optimality in Standard Quadratic Programs with the GOE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18620)
*Xin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了当目标矩阵来自高斯正交系综（GOE）时，单纯形上的标准二次优化问题。证明了全局最优解的支持大小大于1的概率渐近等于 \(2\sqrt{2π}\,\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}\)，即最小对角顶点以趋于1的概率为全局最优，并给出了显式的一阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机二次优化问题中全局最优解的结构特性，特别是支持大小（即活跃约束个数）的分布行为，揭示随机矩阵背景下最优解的稀疏性与相变现象。

Method: 结合了精确的两坐标边缘改进条件与通过对角顺序统计量条件化得到的产品公式。利用边界层估计识别主要贡献，并证明支持大小至少为3的情况可忽略不计。

Result: 证明了 \(\Prob(κ_n>1)\sim 2\sqrt{2π}\,\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}\)，即全局最优解几乎必然为单一支持（最小对角顶点），支持大于1的概率以 \(O(\sqrt{\log n}/n)\) 衰减。

Conclusion: 在GOE矩阵下，标准二次规划的最优解以趋于1的概率是单点支撑的（即最小对角顶点），并给出了非平凡概率的精确一阶渐近表达式。

Abstract: We study the standard quadratic optimization problem over the simplex when the objective matrix is drawn from the Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE). Let \(κ_n\) denote the support size of the almost surely unique global optimizer. We prove \[ \Prob(κ_n>1)\sim 2\sqrt{2π}\,\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}. \] The proof combines an exact two-coordinate condition for edge improvement with a product formula obtained by conditioning on the diagonal order statistics. Boundary-layer estimates identify the leading contribution and show that supports of size at least three are negligible. Consequently, the minimum-diagonal vertex is globally optimal with probability tending to one, with an explicit first-order correction.

</details>


### [190] [Robust Optimization Under Objective Functional Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18669)
*Yue Song,Yuxi Lu,Gang Li,Kairui Feng,Qi Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的鲁棒优化框架（ObRO），通过交替优化算法和分段线性化近似，解决了目标函数在连续函数空间中的不确定性优化问题，并应用于配电网电池充电调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒优化方法主要处理参数不确定性，但实际工程问题中目标函数本身可能存在不确定性（如退化成本函数未知），亟需一种能够在连续函数空间中考虑目标函数不确定性的鲁棒优化框架。

Method: 采用min-max结构，内层在连续函数空间中寻找最坏情况目标函数，外层在欧几里得空间中寻找最优决策；设计交替生成最坏情况目标函数与最优决策的求解算法；基于算子理论证明算法收敛到“半全局”鞍点；提出基于分段线性化（PWL）近似的数值求解器并证明数值一致性。

Result: 证明了交替优化算法收敛到ObRO问题的半全局鞍点；证明了PWL近似问题与原ObRO问题的数值一致性；将所提方法应用于考虑退化效应的配电网电池充电调度问题。

Conclusion: ObRO框架有效解决了目标函数不确定下的鲁棒优化问题，算法具有理论收敛保证，PWL近似方法保证了数值可解性，在电池充电调度中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new robust optimization (RO) formulation namely the RO under objective functional uncertainty (ObRO). The ObRO adopts a min-max structure where the inner problem finds the worst-case objective function in a continuous function space to maximize the cost, and the outer problem finds the optimal decision in a Euclidean space to minimize the cost. A solution algorithm is designed to alternately generate the worst-case objective function at the current decision and the optimal decision for the current collection of objective functions. Using operator theory, we prove that this algorithm converges to the defined ``semi-global'' saddle point of the ObRO problem. In addition, we propose a numerical solver based on the piece-wise linearization (PWL) approximation of objective functions. The PWL approximate problem is proved to be numerically consistent with the original ObRO problem. The obtained results are applied to the degradation-aware battery charging scheduling in distribution networks.

</details>


### [191] [Implementation-Based Incentive Design for Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand and Transit Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18687)
*Xinling Li,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于k-实现理论的替代方法，通过计算最小实现转移支付来协调多模式交通系统中自私的AMoD和公交运营商，避免传统均衡模型的行为假设强、均衡选择难和双层优化复杂等问题。在纽约市案例中，该方法能计算紧致的支付界限，并揭示拥堵如何改变激励失调的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模式交通系统中，AMoD和公交运营商追求自私目标，加上乘客内生选择，使得实现社会期望的运营点极具挑战性。现有基于均衡的监管模型依赖强行为假设、面临均衡选择问题，且求解双层优化困难。

Method: 采用k-实现理论，将所需支付分解为AMoD和公交两方单边偏离增益之和。构建三个计算模块：社会目标（分解+熵正则化混合整数凸规划）、AMoD最优响应（精确重构+凸松弛上界+序贯凸近似下界）、PT最优响应（混合整数凸松弛+精确性条件）。

Result: 纽约市案例表明该框架能计算紧致的实现支付界限，揭示拥堵如何改变激励失调的主要来源。

Conclusion: 基于k-实现理论的协调框架为多模式交通系统监管提供了可行的实施路径，避免了传统均衡方法的多重困难。

Abstract: Achieving a socially desirable operating point for a multimodal transportation system is challenging when Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand (AMoD) and Public Transit (PT) operators pursue selfish objectives alongside endogenous passenger choices. Existing equilibrium-based regulation models typically search over municipal policies to predict the induced operator equilibrium, creating strong behavioral assumptions, equilibrium-selection issues, and difficult bilevel optimization problems. This paper proposes an implementation-based alternative. Rather than asking which municipal action induces the best equilibrium, we ask: given a target operating profile, what minimum realized transfer makes unilateral deviation unattractive for each operator? Using k-implementation theory, this payment decomposes into two unilateral deviation gains: one for the AMoD operator and one for PT. Calculating this payment requires computing three distinct objects: the social target, the AMoD best response, and the PT best response. This is nontrivial because each represents a large-scale network optimization problem complicated by endogenous mode choice and congestion. To address this, we develop tailored mathematical formulations and algorithms for each oracle. For the social target, we derive a decomposition and entropy-regularized mixed-integer convex formulation balancing social optimality and implementability. For the AMoD oracle, we derive an exact reformulation, a convex relaxation providing a global upper bound, and a sequential convex approximation for feasible lower bounds. For the PT oracle, we develop a mixed-integer convex relaxation and characterize its exactness condition. A NYC case study shows the framework computes tight implementation-payment bounds and reveals how the dominant source of incentive misalignment shifts with congestion.

</details>


### [192] [Can Adaptive Gradient Methods Converge under Heavy-Tailed Noise? A Case Study of AdaGrad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18694)
*Zijian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次证明自适应梯度方法AdaGrad在重尾噪声（尾指数4/3<p≤2）下的非凸优化收敛性，无需任何额外裁剪或归一化操作，并给出该算法下界及AdaGrad-Norm的改进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际机器学习优化过程中常出现重尾梯度噪声，尽管梯度裁剪/归一化可保证收敛，但自适应方法（如Adam/AdamW）即使不采用这些操作仍表现良好。本文旨在回答自适应梯度方法是否能在不修改算法的情况下应对重尾噪声，并以AdaGrad为起点进行理论探究。

Method: 针对AdaGrad在非凸光滑设置下，假设随机梯度具有重尾分布（p阶矩有限），通过精细的矩分析和步长自适应特性，首次推导出收敛速率；同时构建算法依赖的下界证明最优率的不可达性；对AdaGrad-Norm变体在额外温和假设下给出更优的收敛保证。

Result: AdaGrad在尾指数4/3<p≤2时无需知晓p值即可保证收敛速率；证明了现有重尾优化极小最优率对AdaGrad不可达；AdaGrad-Norm在1<p≤2时取得改进速率。

Conclusion: 自适应梯度方法（AdaGrad）天然具备抵抗重尾噪声的能力，但存在算法依赖的下界限制；不同变体（如AdaGrad-Norm）可进一步扩展收敛保证范围。

Abstract: Many tasks in modern machine learning are observed to involve heavy-tailed gradient noise during the optimization process. To manage this realistic and challenging setting, new mechanisms, such as gradient clipping and gradient normalization, have been introduced to ensure the convergence of first-order algorithms. However, adaptive gradient methods, a famous class of modern optimizers that includes popular $\mathtt{Adam}$ and $\mathtt{AdamW}$, often perform well even without any extra operations mentioned above. It is therefore natural to ask whether adaptive gradient methods can converge under heavy-tailed noise without any algorithmic changes. In this work, we take the first step toward answering this question by investigating a special case, $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$, the origin of adaptive gradient methods. We provide the first provable convergence rate for $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$ in non-convex optimization when the tail index $p$ satisfies $4/3<p\leq2$. Notably, this result is achieved without requiring any prior knowledge of $p$ and is hence adaptive to the tail index. In addition, we develop an algorithm-dependent lower bound, suggesting that the existing minimax rate for heavy-tailed optimization is not attainable by $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$. Lastly, we consider $\mathtt{AdaGrad}\text{-}\mathtt{Norm}$, a popular variant of $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$ in theoretical studies, and show an improved rate that holds for any $1<p\leq2$ under an extra mild assumption.

</details>
